Experiments on VideoDR and VideoRover-Bench show that our VideoRover-8B-RL achieves performance comparable to proprietary models in the direct-answer setting without tool use while outperforming larger open-source models equipped with the same tool suite. Ablation studies and training dynamics further validate the complementary roles of active video grounding, external retrieval, and long-horizon reinforcement learning.

</details>


### [69] [IntentQA: Intent Question Answering in Videos by Cognitive Context Reasoning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.23330)
*Jiapeng Li,Ping Wei,Wenjuan Han,Song-Chun Zhu,Lifeng Fan*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出IntentQA任务及大规模VideoQA数据集，用于理解人类行为背后的意图（社会智能的"暗物质"）。论文还引入五种LLM生成的对照集和"Contrast Performance Decline"指标来评估模型鲁棒性，并提出了X-CaVIR框架，利用情境、对比和常识三种认知上下文增强视频意图推理，同时通过透明管线整合LLM使推理过程可解释。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统视频理解仅关注视觉表面的识别，缺少对人类行为背后意图的推理能力。此外，标准评估指标可能因数据集偏差而高估模型能力，且现有黑盒模型缺乏可解释性。

Method: 1) 引入IntentQA任务和大规模VideoQA数据集；2) 通过LLM生成五种对照集，提出"Contrast Performance Decline"指标评估鲁棒性；3) 提出X-CaVIR框架，由三个模块构成：基于跨模态VQL的情境上下文模块、基于对比学习的对比上下文模块、基于常识推理的常识上下文模块；4) 采用透明管线将视频字幕与VQA模型输出协同整合，使LLM推理过程可解释。

Result: 大量实验表明各组件有效性，X-CaVIR在多个SOTA基线中表现优异，且在对照集扰动下保持稳定。

Conclusion: X-CaVIR通过融合多种认知上下文和多模块协同设计，在视频意图推理任务中实现了性能与可解释性的平衡，为智能体理解人类意图及模型鲁棒性评估提供了新的范式。

Abstract: Video understanding requires intelligent agents to transcend mere recognition of visual facts and comprehend the underlying intents behind human actions (often termed the "dark matter" of social intelligence). To bridge the gap between visual observation and intent reasoning, we introduce a novel task, IntentQA, and contribute a large-scale VideoQA dataset specifically tailored for this purpose. However, recognizing that standard metrics may overestimate capabilities due to dataset biases, we go beyond simple accuracy to rigorously evaluate model robustness. We augment the benchmark by generating five distinct contrast sets via Large Language Models (LLMs) and introducing a "Contrast Performance Decline" metric. We propose the X-CaVIR (eXplainable Context-aware Video Intent Reasoning) framework, which leverages three types of "Cognitive Context" to enhance video analysis: i) Situational Context via a cross-modal Video Query Language (VQL) module, ii) Contrastive Context via a Contrastive Learning module, and iii) Commonsense Context via a Commonsense Reasoning module. Crucially, to overcome the opacity of traditional black-box models, we refine the integration of LLMs within X-CaVIR by employing a transparent pipeline that synergizes video captions with VQA model outputs. This approach not only improves performance by effectively utilizing rich commonsense knowledge but also renders the reasoning process explicitly interpretable. Extensive experiments demonstrate the effectiveness of our components, the superiority of X-CaVIR over state-of-the-art baselines, and its stability against perturbations on the contrast sets.

</details>


### [70] [Controllable blind deblurring with diffusion models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.23343)
*Imane Si Salah,Emile Cribelier,Thomas Veit,Wolf Hauser,Arthur Leclaire*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出SuperSharpen，一种基于扩散模型的盲去模糊方法，通过模糊度量实现恢复强度的显式控制，并对比了ControlNet风格适配器与全微调两种条件化策略。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 专业摄影中的盲去模糊是一个挑战性逆问题，因为未知的各向同性模糊核导致高频信息丢失。现有生成模型在产生既逼真又忠实于输入的细节方面存在困难，需要一种既能保持照片真实感又能忠实于原图的方法。

Method: 提出SuperSharpen，一种基于扩散模型的盲去模糊方法。通过模糊度量（blur measure）显式控制恢复强度，并对比了两种条件化策略：在冻结backbone上使用ControlNet风格适配器，以及对扩散先验进行全微调。

Result: 实验表明，全微调策略比ControlNet风格适配器具有更好的保真度且产生更少的幻觉细节。在合成和真实模糊数据集上验证了该方法，展示了改进的感知质量和可控的恢复强度。

Conclusion: SuperSharpen通过模糊度量提供显式的恢复强度控制，其中全微调优于适配器策略，在感知质量和保真度之间取得了更好的平衡。

Abstract: Image acquisition with a camera involves several degradations due to the optical system, sensor, or low-level processing steps. We address blind deblurring in professional photography: we aim to invert unknown isotropic blur without knowledge of the degradation kernel.For such inverse problems,where some high-frequency information is lost, it is challenging to use generative models to produce details that are both photo-realistic and faithful to the input. We propose SuperSharpen, a diffusion-based blind deblurring method offering explicit control over restoration strength through a blur measure. We compare two conditioning strategies: a ControlNet-style adapter on a frozen backbone, and full finetuning of the diffusion prior. Our experiments show that finetuning achieves better fidelity with fewer hallucinated details. We validate our approach on synthetic and real-world blur, demonstrating improved perceptual quality and controllable restoration strength.

</details>


### [71] [DF-MoE: Generalizable Deepfake Detection via Multimodal Sparse Mixture-of-Experts](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.23363)
*Vlad Hondru,Florinel Alin Croitoru,Iuliana Georgescu,A. Sophia Koepke,Radu Tudor Ionescu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出DF-MoE框架，利用多种预训练模型提取丰富的高层音视频特征（嘴部运动、人脸解析、面部表情、头部姿态、视线追踪、心率、音频情绪和语音活动），并通过Mixture-of-Experts（MoE）主干网络融合单模态和多模态线索进行深度伪造检测。在五个基准数据集（MAVOS-DD, AVLips, PolyGlotFake, BioDeepAV, FakeAVCeleb）上进行域内和跨域实验，DF-MoE超越了所有现有最先进方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 音视频深度伪造检测的主要挑战是开发能够跨伪造生成方法泛化的检测器。作者推测通过预训练模型从音频和视觉模态中提取多种高层线索可以缓解过拟合问题。

Method: 1) 组装多种预训练模型提取八类高层特征：嘴部运动、人脸解析、面部表情、头部姿态、视线追踪、心率、音频情绪和语音活动；2) 通过Mixture-of-Experts（MoE）主干网络整合单模态和多模态线索进行深度伪造检测。

Result: 在五个数据集上进行域内和跨域实验，DF-MoE取得了优越的深度伪造检测结果，超越了所有竞争方法。

Conclusion: 通过提取多样化高层语义线索并利用MoE架构进行多模态融合，能够有效提升深度伪造检测器的泛化能力，DF-MoE在多个基准上达到了SOTA性能。

Abstract: Audio-visual deepfake detection is an actively studied topic, where one of the main challenges is to develop detectors able to generalize across deepfake generation methods. We conjecture that overfitting can be mitigated by extracting multiple high-level cues from the available audio and visual modalities via pre-trained models. We therefore assemble a wide variety of pre-trained models to extract features that encode mouth movements, face parsing, facial expressions, head pose, gaze tracking, heart rate, audio emotion and speech activity. We further integrate both unimodal and multimodal cues via a Mixture-of-Experts (MoE) backbone to detect deepfakes. We perform in-domain and cross-domain experiments on five benchmarks for deepfake detection (MAVOS-DD, AVLips, PolyGlotFake, BioDeepAV, FakeAVCeleb) to compare our framework (DF-MoE) with state-of-the-art methods. Our results indicate that DF-MoE obtains superior deepfake detection results, surpassing all competing methods. We release our code at https://github.com/vladhondru25/DF-MoE.

</details>


### [72] [Long-Horizon Audio-Visual Generation for Persistent Stories and Interactive Worlds](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.23383)
*Nan Duan,Haoyang Huang,Weiyang Jin,Haoran Li,Yaowei Li,Yuming Li,Yijun Liu,Xin Lu,Xiaoxiao Ma,Yanwen Ma,Yaofeng Su,Yilang Sun,Haoyu Wang,Zeyue Xue,Songchun Zhang,Junhao Zhuang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出了JoyAI-Echo-1.5，一个统一的音视频生成系统，包含长视频和世界模型两个变体，分别通过可组合的跨镜头记忆和几何感知的相机轨迹条件化实现长时叙事生成与交互式世界模拟，并设计了渐进的教师强制和自回归梯度强制训练策略支持高效的长时序生成，在多个基准上取得了领先性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 视频生成正从孤立片段向长叙事和交互式世界演进，现有模型难以在长时间展开中保持身份一致、响应用户控制并保持生成稳定性，亟需一个统一的框架同时解决上述跨模态、跨时域和交互控制问题。

Method: 论文提出两个变体：(1) 长视频变体，引入可组合的跨镜头记忆聚合多镜头视觉证据，并结合语音过滤的全镜头音频提取说话人线索，支持文本、图像、记忆的灵活条件组合；(2) 世界模型变体，将异构导航输入转换为标定的度量6-DoF相机轨迹，通过几何感知条件通路注入，实现跨控制器和视点的交互。训练方面，将双向音频-视觉骨干转化为因果少步生成器，采用渐进教师强制和短/长时域自梯度强制在自生成rollout上训练。

Result: 长视频变体在跨镜头一致性、视觉质量、文本对齐和语音保真度上均优于现有长视频基线；世界模型变体在WBench上排名第一（平均分81.7），并在SANA-WM-Bench上取得领先的视觉质量和长时间持续性。

Conclusion: 记忆机制、几何控制和rollout感知训练为生成连贯故事和持续演化的交互世界提供了实用基础，证明了上述三要素在统一音视频生成框架中的有效性。

Abstract: Video generation is progressing beyond isolated clips toward long-form narratives and interactive worlds, requiring models to preserve identities, follow user controls, and remain stable over extended rollouts. We present JoyAI-Echo-1.5, a unified audio-visual generation system with two purpose-built variants. The long-video variant introduces composable cross-shot memory that aggregates visual evidence across multiple prior shots and speaker cues derived from speech-filtered full-shot audio, enabling persistent character appearance and voice identity across flexible combinations of text, image, and memory conditioning. The world-model variant converts heterogeneous navigation inputs into calibrated metric 6-DoF camera trajectories and injects them through a geometry-aware conditioning pathway, enabling controller-agnostic interaction across flexible viewpoints. To support efficient long-horizon generation, we transform a bidirectional audio-visual backbone into a causal few-step generator using progressive teacher forcing and short- and long-horizon Self-Gradient Forcing on self-generated rollouts. Experiments demonstrate strong performance in both settings. JoyAI-Echo-1.5 achieves improvements over existing long-video baselines in cross-shot consistency, visual quality, text alignment, and speech fidelity. Its world-model variant ranks first on WBench, with an average score of 81.7, and achieves leading visual quality and long-horizon persistence on SANA-WM-Bench. Together, these results indicate that memory, geometric control, and rollout-aware training provide a practical foundation for generating coherent stories and continuously evolving interactive worlds. Project page: https://echo-team-joy-future-academy-jd.github.io/Echo-1.5-Page/.

</details>


### [73] [Photorealistic Novel View Synthesis of Human Faces using Next-Scale Transformers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.23410)
*Federico Stella,Fei Jiang,Zhongshi Jiang,Zohar Barzelay,Emanuel Garbin,Amin Jourabloo,Liuhao Ge*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于next-scale自回归范式的人脸多视角新视角合成方法，支持高分辨率、多视角输出和跨视角一致性，并在合成人脸数据集上训练。该方法无需2D预训练，可利用低分辨率通用预训练，对特定数据需求量小，并能与3D高斯提升模型结合生成逼真的3D人脸模型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 高分辨率、多相机条件下的人像新视角合成仍然面临严峻挑战，核心难点在于保持身份一致性、精细外观细节和几何连贯性。现有方法（如扩散模型）依赖大量2D预训练和特定数据，难以在有限数据下高效收敛并保证跨视角一致性。

Method: 基于next-scale自回归范式构建人像视角合成模型：1) 扩展现有next-scale架构以支持更高分辨率和多视角输出；2) 通过next-scale结构利用低分辨率通用预训练，仅在最后训练阶段使用全分辨率特定数据；3) 在涵盖多样身份与服饰的合成人脸数据集上训练，实现单次前向传播同时生成多个视角；4) 将输出与基于transformer的像素对齐3D高斯提升模型结合，生成精确逼真的3D人脸模型。

Result: 实验表明，该方法在人像主体上取得了感知保真度和跨视角一致性的提升，生成的视角清晰逼真，且支持同时合成多个新颖视角以改善视角间的一致性。与3D高斯提升模型耦合后，能够生成准确且照片级真实的3D人脸模型。

Conclusion: next-scale自回归范式是人像多视角合成中可扩展、多输出的有效骨干网络，相较于扩散模型在数据效率和跨视角一致性方面具有优势，为高保真人像新视角合成提供了新思路。

Abstract: Photorealistic novel view synthesis of people remains challenging at high spatial resolutions and across multiple target cameras, where preserving identity, fine appearance details, and geometric coherence is critical. We build on the next-scale autoregressive paradigm and adapt it for human-centric view synthesis by enabling higher image resolutions, multi-view outputs and stronger cross-view consistency in a single forward pass. We train on a synthetic dataset of human faces spanning diverse identities and apparel. Contrary to diffusion models, this paradigm does not need 2D pre-training and, thanks to its next-scale architecture, it benefits from lower-resolution, general-purpose pre-trainings, with the full-sized purpose-specific images being used only in the last training stages. This enables our architecture to converge with a smaller amount of purpose-specific training data, allowing us to use a smaller but more realistic training dataset. The resulting model produces sharp and realistic views, with the option to synthesize multiple novel viewpoints simultaneously for improved agreement across views. Empirically, we observe gains in perceptual fidelity and cross-view coherence on human subjects, demonstrating that next-scale autoregression is an effective backbone for scalable, multi-output human view synthesis. We also couple our pipeline with an existing transformer-based model for pixel-aligned 3D gaussian lifting from multi-view facial inputs, resulting in accurate and photorealistic 3D models of human faces.

</details>


### [74] [Image-Conditioned Diffusion Models for Quality Assurance of Organ-at-Risk Segmentations in Radiotherapy](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.23432)
*Clea Dronne,Catharine H Clark,Xavier Loizeau,Elizabeth Miles,Peter Hoskin,Jamie R McClelland*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 该研究比较了VAE框架与图像条件分割扩散模型在头颈部CT放疗中器官危及区（OAR）分割错误检测中的表现，发现扩散模型在定位细微边界错误方面更一致，支持其作为局部解剖感知分割QA的可行框架。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 放疗计划中准确的OAR分割至关重要，但人工审查分割结果耗时且主观，亟需自动化的分割错误检测方法以提高QA效率和客观性。

Method: 采用规范建模方法，对比VAE框架与图像条件分割扩散模型，在RADCURE数据集上利用模拟的边界和宽度扰动评估脑干与脊髓分割错误检测性能；错误检测通过输入分割与重建分割之间的Dice相似系数和距离协议（DTA）进行量化。

Result: 两个模型均能检测部分模拟错误，但区域级DTA分析显示扩散模型在定位细微边界错误方面表现更一致。

Conclusion: 图像条件扩散重建是用于局部化、解剖感知分割QA的有前景框架，在头颈部CT分割错误检测中优于VAE方法。

Abstract: Accurate organ-at-risk segmentation is essential for radiotherapy planning, but reviewing segmentations is time-consuming and subjective. We investigate normative modelling for segmentation error detection in head-and-neck CT, comparing a VAE framework with an image-conditioned segmentation diffusion model. Models were evaluated on RADCURE brainstem and spinal cord segmentations using simulated boundary and width perturbations. Error detection was assessed using the Dice similarity coefficient and the Distance to Agreement (DTA) between the input and reconstructed segmentations. While both models detected some simulated errors, regional DTA showed that the diffusion model localised subtle boundary errors more consistently. These results support image-conditioned diffusion reconstruction as a promising framework for localised, anatomy-aware segmentation QA.

</details>


### [75] [Towards Comprehensive Basketball Understanding](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.23435)
*Yirong Hu,Jiayuan Rao,Yu Zhang,Shangzhe Di,Weidi Xie*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出BasketballBench，一个包含7,980个问题、覆盖文本/图像/视频三种模态、跨越十项任务的篮球多模态基准，以及BasketballSkills智能体，后者通过组合八个领域专用工具和四个可复用技能，显著提升了多能力整合场景下的篮球理解表现。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有篮球理解基准通常单独评估事件识别、动作定位、球员识别和结构化游戏知识等能力，未充分探索这些能力之间的协同交互，缺乏综合性的多模态基准。

Method: 构建基于2025-2026 NBA赛季的BasketballBench基准（含官方逐回合数据、530名活跃球员档案和2,501个回合级转播片段），并提出BasketballSkills智能体，组合八个篮球专用感知与检索工具，归纳为四个可复用技能，每个技能明确了工具调用顺序、证据绑定和终止条件。

Result: 实验表明现有多模态大语言模型在需要多能力整合的问题上表现不佳，而BasketballSkills在所有任务中优于这些基线模型，验证了显式组合领域专用能力的有效性。

Conclusion: 显式地组合多种领域专用能力是全面理解篮球比赛的有效途径，BasketballBench为未来篮球多模态理解研究提供了更全面的评估平台。

Abstract: Understanding a basketball game requires recognizing events, localizing actions, identifying players, and relating these to structured game knowledge. Existing benchmarks primarily evaluate these abilities one at a time, leaving the interactions among these abilities under-explored. We introduce BasketballBench, a multimodal benchmark comprising 7,980 questions across ten tasks in text, image, and video. It is built from the 2025-2026 NBA season and includes official playby-play, rosters and profiles for 530 active players, and 2,501 possession-level broadcast clips. We further propose BasketballSkills, an agent that composes eight basketball-specific perception and retrieval tools under four reusable skills that specify tool order, evidence bindings, and stopping conditions. Experiments show that current MLLMs struggle particularly on questions requiring the integration of multiple capabilities, whereas BasketballSkills outperforms them, highlighting the effectiveness of explicitly composing domain-specific capabilities for comprehensive basketball understanding.

</details>


### [76] [Geometry-Driven Opti-Acoustic Co-Registration and View-Invariant Reflectivity Mapping for Side-Scan Sonar](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.23479)
*Taqi Hamoda,Nuno Gracias*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于几何驱动的框架，利用SfM重建的3D海底网格作为光学与声学域之间的几何锚点，实现像素级的光学-声学图像配准和视角不变的反射率制图，消除了人工标注需求。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 侧扫声呐（SSS）是水下大规模测绘的主要模态，但声学复杂性（散斑噪声、阴影、极端视角依赖）严重阻碍了自动化感知和跨模态对齐。传统手工特征描述子和现代深度学习匹配器在没有3D几何约束的情况下，无法弥合光学与声学图像之间的物理域差距。

Method: 该框架包含三个核心组件：(1) 利用Structure-from-Motion (SfM)重建密集3D海底网格作为光学与声学域之间的几何锚点；(2) 提出First Bottom Return (FBR)提取算法，动态校正SfM未标定重建导致的非线性高度漂移；(3) 应用逆Lambertian模型和双高斯加权函数分离固有海底反射率，中和斜距传播损耗和几何视角依赖性，最后将分离的声学属性与光学像素确定性关联。

Result: 该流水线生成了高精度、严格共配准的多模态数据集，实现了自动化且物理引导的处理流程，消除了对人工标注的依赖，为海底栖息地制图中的高级自监督学习铺平了道路。

Conclusion: 这项工作通过物理引导、几何约束的方式弥合了光学与声学域之间的差距，提供了一种无需人工标注的自动跨模态配准解决方案，有望推动水下多模态感知和自监督学习的发展。

Abstract: Side-Scan Sonar (SSS) is a primary modality for large-scale underwater mapping, yet automated perception and cross-modal alignment are severely bottlenecked by acoustic complexities such as speckle noise, shadows, and extreme viewpoint dependencies. Traditional handcrafted descriptors and modern deep learning matchers fail to bridge the physical domain gap between optical and acoustic imagery without 3D geometric constraints. To overcome these limitations, we propose a novel geometry-driven framework for pixel-level opti-acoustic co-registration and view-invariant reflectivity mapping. Our method utilizes Structure-from-Motion (SfM) to reconstruct a dense 3D seafloor mesh, acting as a geometric anchor between the visual and acoustic domains. We introduce a First Bottom Return (FBR) extraction algorithm to dynamically correct non-linear altitude drift caused by uncalibrated SfM reconstruction. Furthermore, we apply an inverse Lambertian model and a dual-Gaussian weighting function to isolate the intrinsic seabed reflectivity, effectively neutralizing slant-range propagation loss and geometric view-dependence. By deterministically associating these isolated acoustic properties with optical pixels, our pipeline generates highly accurate, strictly co-registered multi-modal datasets. This automated, physics-guided approach eliminates the need for manual annotation and paves the way for advanced self-supervised learning in benthic habitat mapping.

</details>


### [77] [SVD-Based Typicality Maps for Out-of-Distribution Detection in Vision Transformers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.23499)
*Aldo Sean Sartor,Leandro de Souza Rosa,Andriy Enttsel,Mauro Mangia,Riccardo Rovatti*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出一种基于参数几何分析的Vision Transformer内部表征分析方法，通过SVD分解权重矩阵、投影激活值到主右奇异向量，构建逐层类条件密度模型生成"典型性图"，并由此衍生出两种后验OOD检测分数（PAS和MLSV），在不重训练、不接触OOD数据的情况下取得有竞争力的检测性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有ViT内部表征分析方法忽视了参数空间的几何结构，且OOD检测方法往往需要重训练或接触OOD数据，缺乏一种不依赖额外训练且能深入利用网络层间信息的轻量级解决方案。

Method: 1) 对每个仿射层的权重矩阵进行SVD分解；2) 将激活投影到前导右奇异向量上获得紧凑的层内表征；3) 在每层拟合类条件密度模型，计算每个类的典型性分数；4) 将跨层分数堆叠成典型性图；5) 从典型性图衍生两种OOD检测分数：PAS（原型对齐分数，度量与类参考原型的吻合度）和MLSV（多层软投票分数，捕获跨层共识）。

Result: 在CIFAR-100上微调的ViT-B/16上，所提出的PAS和MLSV分数在不需重训练或OOD暴露的情况下取得了具有竞争力的OOD检测性能。

Conclusion: 通过利用ViT权重的几何结构，可以在不额外训练的情况下有效解释类证据在网络深度中的演化，并实现可靠的OOD检测，为可解释性和安全性提供了一种轻量级后验工具。

Abstract: We present a method for analyzing the internal representations of Vision Transformers (ViTs) exploiting the geometry of their learned parameters. Each affine layer's weight matrix is factored via Singular Value Decomposition (SVD), and activations are projected onto the leading right singular vectors to obtain compact, layer-intrinsic representations. A class-conditional density model is then fitted at each layer, producing per-class \emph{typicality scores} that are stacked across depth into \emph{typicality maps}: two-dimensional summaries of how class-specific evidence evolves through the network. From these maps, we derive two post-hoc scores for Out-Of-Distribution (OOD) detection: a \emph{Prototype Alignment Score} (PAS), measuring agreement with class reference prototype patterns, and a \emph{Multi-Layer Soft Voting} (MLSV) score, capturing cross-layer consensus without stored prototypes. On ViT-B/16 fine-tuned on CIFAR-100, the proposed scores achieve competitive detection performance without retraining or OOD exposure.

</details>


### [78] [Action-Aligned Retrieval with Pairwise Multimodal Reranking for Text-Based Person Anomaly Search](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.23503)
*Thanh-Khoi Nguyen,Thanh-Nhan Vo,Trong-Thuan Nguyen,Minh-Triet Tran*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出ActPair，一个三阶段粗到细框架，用于文本行人异常行为检索。该框架结合动作对齐的VLM微调、并行晚期融合检索（原始查询+LLM上下文改写）以及基于pivot-promote算法的成对多模态重排序，在PAB公开测试集上取得最佳性能，并能有效迁移到非异常特定数据集。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有文本行人异常行为检索方法存在三方面局限：依赖孤立的骨骼几何特征而忽略上下文条件化动作；查询改写过程中丢失原始查询细节；多模态验证采用绝对逐点打分，无法处理细粒度、上下文依赖的行为判别。这些方法难以弥合姿态语义与自然语言描述之间的鸿沟。

Method: ActPair包含三个核心阶段：(1) 使用动作对齐的多任务目标微调视觉语言模型（VLM），使表征编码动作判别性语义；(2) 并行晚期融合检索，同时使用原始查询和LLM生成的上下文补齐改写，保留两种语义视角的互补细节；(3) 提出基于pivot-promote算法的即插即用重排序模块，通过直接成对视觉比较消除残余的空间和组合歧义，避免穷举评估带来的高昂推理成本。

Result: 在Pedestrian Anomaly Behavior (PAB)公开测试集上，相较于对比方法取得最佳结果；同时有效迁移至一个未见过的非异常特定数据集，展示了良好的泛化能力。

Conclusion: ActPair通过动作对齐表征学习、双视角查询融合检索和高效成对重排序三个阶段的协同设计，有效解决了文本行人异常行为检索中姿态语义鸿沟问题，在准确性和推理效率之间取得了良好平衡，并展现出跨数据集的泛化能力。

Abstract: Text-based person anomaly search requires distinguishing individuals based on fine-grained, context-dependent behaviors rather than mere appearance. Existing methods struggle to capture these context-conditioned actions, frequently relying on isolated skeletal geometry, discarding raw query details during reformulation, or utilizing absolute pointwise scoring for multimodal verification. To address these limitations, we propose \textbf{ActPair}, a unified three-stage coarse-to-fine framework that combines action-aligned retrieval with pairwise multimodal reranking to bridge the pose-semantic gap. First, we fine-tune a vision-language model (VLM) with an action-aligned multi-task objective that encourages the representations to encode action-discriminative semantics. Second, we perform parallel late-fusion retrieval using the original query and a large language model (LLM)-generated context-grounded rewrite, retaining complementary details from both semantic views. Finally, we propose an efficient off-the-shelf reranking module that leverages a pivot-promote algorithm to perform direct pairwise visual comparisons, mitigating residual spatial and compositional ambiguities without the prohibitive inference costs of exhaustive evaluation. Extensive experiments demonstrate that our framework achieves the best results among the compared methods on the Pedestrian Anomaly Behavior (PAB) public test and transfers effectively to an unseen, non-anomaly-specific dataset.

</details>


### [79] [Investigating Relational Reasoning in VLMs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.23518)
*Adhithya Laxman Ravi Shankar Geetha,Aulia Kharis Rakhmasari,Haleema Ramzan,Xander Yap*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本研究通过合成几何形状数据集和精确设计的语言提示查询，分析了Qwen3-VL-4B视觉语言模型中视觉信息在深度上的编码方式，发现现代VLM同时结合了真正的视觉推理和基于语言线索的捷径策略。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 虽然VLM在视觉推理任务中表现优异，但研究者并不清楚它们是否真正理解视觉关系，还是仅仅利用语言线索或先验知识等捷径来完成推理。为了探究这一问题，需要设计受控实验来解码VLM内部的信息编码机制。

Method: 构建了一个由简单几何形状组成的合成数据集用于受控分析，并精心设计查询来精确测试语言线索的影响。此外，通过修改数据集来测试模型对视觉证据的因果依赖性，并利用Qwen3-VL-4B这一现代VLM解码视觉信息在不同网络深度上的编码方式。

Result: 结果显示，现代VLMs将真正的视觉推理与主要以语言线索为基础的捷径策略相结合，即在推理过程中既会利用真实的视觉证据，也会依赖语言相关信息的捷径。

Conclusion: 当前VLM的视觉推理并非纯粹基于视觉理解，而是融合了视觉证据与语言线索的混合策略，这一发现对理解VLM推理机制及改进其可靠性具有重要意义。

Abstract: Vision-Language Models (VLMs) achieve strong performance in visual reasoning tasks, but it remains unclear whether they understand visual relations, or simply employ shortcuts such as language cues or priors. To investigate this, we use the Qwen3-VL-4B (Bai et al., 2025), a modern VLM, to decode how visual information is encoded across depths. For this, we propose a synthetic dataset of simple geometric shapes for controlled analysis, along with queries crafted to precisely test language cues. Furthermore, the dataset is modified to test causal reliance on visual evidence. Our results show that current VLMs combine genuine visual reasoning with shortcut strategies primarily rooted in language cues.

</details>


### [80] [Predicting Multiple Clinical Outcomes Related to Functional Recovery and Social Isolation Among Older Adults After Lower-Limb Fracture or Hip Replacement](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.23531)
*Santosh Ray,Pratik K. Mishra,Ali Abedi,Charlene H. Chu,Amir Ahmad,Shehroz S. Khan*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: Summary generation failed


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Motivation analysis unavailable

Method: Method extraction failed

Result: Result analysis unavailable

Conclusion: Conclusion extraction failed

Abstract: Older adults recovering after lower-limb fracture or hip replacement may experience complex recovery trajectories. Most of the time, these clinical aspects are studied in isolation, masking their joint impact on recovery. This study used the MAISON-LLF dataset, which contains multimodal sensor and clinical assessment data from 18 older adults recovering in the community after lower-limb fracture or hip replacement. Participants were monitored for up to eight weeks, corresponding to a maximum of 1,008 participant-days of sensor monitoring. Forty-six daily features were extracted from indoor motion, acceleration, step count, heart rate, out-of-home mobility, and sleep data. Five clinical outcomes were assessed every two weeks: the Social Isolation Scale, Oxford Hip Score, Oxford Knee Score, Timed Up and Go test, and 30-second Chair Stand test. We utilize an inherent relationship between multi-modal sensor data and different clinical scores and formulate it as a multi-output regression problem. We tested various machine learning and deep learning single- and multi-output regression algorithms to predict these scores simultaneously. The results showed that predicting clinical scores jointly was better than separately. The tabular DL multi-output regressor, NODE, gave a remarkable performance of MSE=3.96 and MAE=1.02 in comparison to other multi- and single-output regressors. The SHAP feature analysis further showed the importance of including multimodal sensors to provide a good estimate of patients' recovery trajectory. This work may support the simultaneous assessment of functional recovery and social engagement among community-dwelling older adults and ultimately help improve their care and quality of life.

</details>


### [81] [EG-ARSA: An Expert-Grounded Open Model for Visual Road Safety Auditing in Low-Resource Settings](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.23563)
*Md Thamed Bin Zaman Chowdhury,Moazzem Hossain*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出专家接地蒸馏（EGD）框架，将机构道路安全专业知识蒸馏到紧凑的视觉语言模型中，实现了可扩展的视觉道路安全审计。通过量化专家接地阶段校准教师模型，并将其结构化监督蒸馏到80亿参数的轻量级学生模型中。同时发布了首个开放、专家接地的孟加拉国道路安全审计数据集BD-ARSA（含21,947条图像审计记录）及专为此任务开发的视觉语言模型EG-ARSA。实验表明该框架在序数风险评估上显著优于零样本基线，且紧凑学生模型超越其310亿参数教师和Gemini-2.5-Flash。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 中低收入国家的道路安全审计面临事故记录不完整、合格审计人员短缺和实地检查成本高昂等挑战，缺乏可扩展的主动式道路安全审计方案。

Method: 提出EGD框架：(1) 量化专家接地阶段，利用权威实地审计校准教师视觉语言模型（Cohen's kappa=0.74后允许大规模标注）；(2) 使用LoRA和单一无泄漏提示将校准教师的结构化监督蒸馏到80亿参数的学生视觉语言模型中；(3) 构建BD-ARSA数据集（21,947条图像审计记录）并开发EG-ARSA模型。

Result: 接地微调显著改善了零样本基线的序数风险评估；盲审专家评估表明80亿参数的紧凑学生模型在性能上优于其310亿参数教师模型和Gemini-2.5-Flash。

Conclusion: EGD为资源受限环境下的主动式道路安全审计提供了有效且可扩展的工程解决方案。

Abstract: Road traffic injuries remain a major challenge in low- and middle-income countries, where proactive road safety auditing is limited by incomplete crash records, shortages of qualified auditors, and the high cost of large-scale field inspections. To address this problem, we propose Expert-Grounded Distillation (EGD), a novel artificial intelligence framework that transfers institutional road safety expertise into a compact vision-language model for scalable visual road safety auditing. The key innovation is a quantified expert-grounding stage in which the teacher vision-language model is calibrated against authoritative field audits. Large-scale annotation is permitted only after the teacher reaches substantial agreement with expert risk assessments (Cohen's kappa = 0.74). The calibrated teacher then generates structured supervision that is distilled into an 8-billion-parameter student vision-language model using Low-Rank Adaptation and a single leakage-free prompt. We also introduce Bangladesh Road Safety Audit (BD-ARSA), the first open, expert-grounded Bangladeshi visual road safety audit dataset containing 21,947 image-audit records with near-national coverage, and Expert-Grounded Road Safety Auditor (EG-ARSA), the first vision-language model developed specifically for this task. Experimental results show that grounded fine-tuning substantially improves ordinal risk assessment over the zero-shot baseline, while blind expert evaluation demonstrates that the compact student outperforms both its 31 billion-parameter teacher and Gemini-2.5-Flash. These findings demonstrate that EGD provides an effective and scalable engineering solution for proactive road safety auditing in resource-constrained environments.

</details>

### [82] [RIACT: A Responsible AI System for Personalized Study Habit Tracking and Early Burnout Signal Detection in University Students](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.21379)
*Ria Sidhu*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文介绍RIACT（Record, Insight, Analyze, Coach, Track），一个结合结构化学习会话记录与混合AI架构的Web应用，用于向大学生提供个性化的学习行为洞察与早期倦怠信号检测。系统通过透明确定性规则进行周际行为对比以检测倦怠信号，并使用受限输出格式的大语言模型生成个性化建议，同时嵌入负责任AI原则（可审计规则、观察而非诊断、最小化数据收集）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 学生倦怠在高等教育中高度普遍（12%至70%以上），且远高于在职人群，但通常只能在学业下滑事后被识别。由于学生缺乏对自身学习行为的结构化可见性，现有生产力工具只记录活动而不进行解读，因此需要一个能主动提供个性化洞察和早期倦怠信号的系统。

Method: RIACT采用混合AI架构：学生按地点和时间记录学习会话；系统通过考虑休息时间计算净专注时间；通过透明、确定性的规则对周际行为进行比较来检测倦怠信号；使用受固定输出模式约束的大语言模型为行为模式提供情境化解释并生成个性化建议。系统设计围绕负责任AI原则：警告由可审计规则而非模型判断控制，所有输出定位为观察而非诊断，数据收集仅限于自我记录的行为字段。

Result: 论文描述了系统的设计原理，将其定位在学生倦怠与教育领域可解释AI相关的文献中，并提出一个评估框架，用于将该系统的行为信号与已建立的倦怠测量工具进行验证对照。

Conclusion: RIACT提出了一种以负责任AI为核心的学习行为监控与倦怠早期预警方案，通过确定性规则与LLM结合的方式在个性化与可解释性之间取得平衡，为教育场景中的学生倦怠主动干预提供了可行的技术路径。

Abstract: Student burnout is highly prevalent in higher education, with reported rates ranging from 12% to over 70% and consistently exceeding those of the working population - yet it is typically identified only retrospectively, after academic decline has already occurred. A contributing factor is that students have little structured visibility into their own study behaviour, and existing productivity tools record activity without interpreting it. This paper presents RIACT (Record, Insight, Analyze, Coach, Track), a web-based application that combines structured study session logging with a hybrid AI architecture to surface personalized insights and early burnout signals. Students log sessions by location and time; the system computes net focus time by accounting for breaks, detects burnout signals through transparent, deterministic rules operating on week-over-week behavioural comparisons, and uses a large language model - constrained to a fixed output schema - to contextualize patterns and generate personalized recommendations. The design embeds responsible AI principles throughout: warnings are governed by auditable rules rather than model judgement, all output is framed as an observation rather than a diagnosis and data collection is limited to self-logged behavioural fields. We describe the system's design rationale, situate it within the literature on student burnout and explainable AI in education and propose an evaluation framework for validating its behavioural signals against established burnout instruments.

</details>


### [83] [Spyre-Accelerated Retrieval-Augmented Generation on IBM LinuxONE: A Cloud-Native Architecture for Secure, High-Throughput Enterprise AI Inference](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.21393)
*Sandeep Bokkasam,Pankaj D*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种完全运行在IBM LinuxONE平台上的六子系统RAG架构，利用Spyre加速器进行生成式推理、Telum II芯片加速器处理轻量分类任务、Red Hat OpenShift进行容器编排，使敏感数据全程不出硬件边界。早期分析显示端到端RAG延迟低于两秒，相比离平台推理可降低20倍延迟，同时保持监管行业所需的加密与可审计性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 企业环境中运行大语言模型面临数据位置与AI算力分离的根本矛盾——敏感数据跨平台迁移会带来延迟、安全和合规方面的风险。作者旨在通过将整个AI推理pipeline置于单一LinuxONE系统内，消除数据外泄隐患，同时满足受监管行业对安全性和可审计性的严格要求。

Method: 设计并实现了一个包含六个子系统的RAG架构：查询接入、向量检索、提示词组装、LLM推理、合规过滤和响应交付。生成推理由Spyre PCIe推理卡承担，轻量分类任务由Telum II片上加速器处理，Red Hat OpenShift负责容器编排。系统利用LinuxONE的Secure Execution技术将机密计算扩展到AI工作负载，并针对Spyre的编译与服务栈进行了深入设计。

Result: 早期基准分析显示：与云GPU和本地替代方案相比，端到端RAG延迟可低于两秒，相对离平台推理方案最多可降低20倍延迟，同时保持了强加密和可审计性特性。

Conclusion: 该架构证明企业可以在单一硬件平台内完成完整的AI推理pipeline，在不牺牲性能的前提下解决数据合规性与安全性痛点，特别适合金融、医疗等受监管行业的本地化AI部署场景。

Abstract: Running large language models inside enterprise environments has always bumped up against a practical wall: the data lives in one place, the AI horsepower sits somewhere else, and moving sensitive records between the two creates real headaches around latency, security, and regulatory exposure. IBM's Spyre accelerator PCIe inference card built for LinuxONE and the broader IBM Z family changes that equation.
  In this paper we lay out a six-subsystem RAG architecture that runs entirely on IBM LinuxONE, using Spyre for generative inference, the Telum II on-chip accelerator for lightweight classification tasks, and Red Hat OpenShift for container orchestration. Every piece of the pipeline from query intake through vector retrieval, prompt assembly, LLM inference, compliance filtering, and response delivery stays within a single LinuxONE system, so sensitive data never has to leave the hardware perimeter.
  We walk through the design choices behind each subsystem, dig into the Spyre compilation and serving stack, explain how LinuxONE's Secure Execution technology extends confidential-computing guarantees to AI workloads, and benchmark the architecture against cloud-GPU and on-premises alternatives. Early analysis points to end-to-end RAG latencies under two seconds and up to a 20x reduction compared to off-platform inference, all while keeping the strong encryption and auditability posture that regulated industries actually need.

</details>


### [84] [Hate Speech Classification In Roman Urdu: A Comparative Study On Parameter Efficient Fine-Tuning And Prompt Engineering](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.21408)
*Toneema Zubair*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本研究旨在识别低资源语言（罗曼乌尔都语）仇恨言论分类的最有效技术，系统比较了提示调优、基于LoRA的参数高效微调（PEFT）和提示工程等方法，通过四个实验在不同配置下评估它们在有限数据场景中的表现。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 由于互联网和社交媒体的普及，仇恨和有毒内容呈指数级增长，造成严重的社会负面影响。罗曼乌尔都语作为一种低资源语言，因其非正式语法、不一致的句子结构和多变的单词拼写，使得仇恨言论分类面临额外挑战。研究旨在在数据有限的低资源环境下找到最有效的仇恨言论分类技术。

Method: 研究设计并实施了四个实验来比较不同方法：(1) 直接推理实验——LLM在零样本设置下直接推理，评估其对罗曼乌尔都语的理解能力；(2) PEFT实验——使用LoRA进行参数高效微调，仅更新少量参数以降低计算成本；(3) 提示调优实验——使用混合和手工设计的提示，配合相对于整个数据集非常小的训练样例集；(4) 提示工程实验——通过零样本和少样本学习，仅依赖精心设计的指令提示进行分类，无需进一步训练。

Result: 论文尚未提供具体实验结果，但通过四种不同的实验配置系统地比较了提示调优、LoRA参数高效微调和提示工程在低资源罗曼乌尔都语仇恨言论分类中的有效性，涵盖零样本、少样本和微调等多种场景。

Conclusion: 该研究为低资源语言环境下仇恨言论分类提供了方法比较框架，旨在确定在数据有限时最有效且计算高效的分类技术方案。

Abstract: Due to the widespread accessibility of the internet and social media, toxic and hateful con-tent has grown exponentially, causing significant distress and negative societal impacts. Ro-man Urdu, a low-resource language used in Pakistan and among Urdu-speaking communities worldwide, presents additional challenges because of its informal grammar, inconsistent sen-tence structures, and multiple variations in word spellings. This research aims to identify the most effective techniques for hate speech classification in such low-resource settings with limited data. To address this, the study investigates and compares the latest approaches, in-cluding prompt tuning, parameter-efficient fine-tuning (PEFT) using LoRA, and prompt en-gineering, under various experimental configurations. To achieve this objective, four exper-iments were designed. The first experiment involved direct inferencing with LLMs without any fine-tuning, to evaluate how well these models understand Roman Urdu in a zero-shot setting, especially given limited data. The second experiment utilized parameter-efficient fine-tuning (PEFT) with LoRA, which updates only a small subset of parameters, thereby reducing computational cost. The third experiment explored prompt tuning with both mixed and manually crafted prompts, using very small sets of training examples relative to the entire dataset, making it computationally efficient as well. Finally, the fourth experiment applied prompt engineering through zero-shot and few-shot learning, relying solely on care-fully designed instruction prompts for classification without further training.

</details>


### [85] [The Abstention Protocol: RCA for Clos Fabrics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.21412)
*Madhava Gaikwad,Deepak Pandey*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: CoreSec是一个生产级根因分析系统，采用PAM风格弃权代数替代加权融合，通过控制标志组合遥测代理，实现确定性决策和显式弃权，在超大规模数据中心网络中提供稳定、可解释的RCA行为。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大型数据中心网络中的根因分析面临遥测数据噪声、部分性和异步性的挑战，基于评分的方法在这些条件下会退化，产生不稳定或错误的归因结果。

Method: CoreSec使用PAM风格的弃权代数替代加权融合方法。遥测代理通过控制标志组合，产生确定性决策，在证据模糊时显式弃权。该系统结合拓扑感知配置，捕获Clos架构中的故障面，且随证据累积单调收敛。

Result: 在超大规模环境中部署，CoreSec无需重新调参即可在不同环境中提供稳定且可解释的RCA行为。

Conclusion: 结构化组合与弃权机制构成真实世界云网络中自动化RCA的实用基础。

Abstract: Root cause analysis (RCA) in large datacenter networks is challenging because telemetry is noisy, partial, and asynchronous. Score-based approaches degrade under these conditions, often yielding unstable or incorrect attributions.
  We present \textsc{CoreSec}, a production RCA system that replaces weighted fusion with a PAM-style abstention algebra. Telemetry agents are composed using control flags that yield deterministic decisions and explicit abstention when evidence is ambiguous. CoreSec combines this algebra with topology-aware configurations that capture failure surfaces across Clos fabrics and converge monotonically as evidence accumulates.
  Deployed at hyperscale, CoreSec provides stable and explainable RCA behavior across diverse environments without retuning. Our experience shows that structured composition with abstention forms a practical foundation for automated RCA in real-world cloud networks.

</details>


### [86] [Retrieval-grounded robot program generation and simulation-based correction via Model Context Protocol](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.21417)
*Zhichao Zhou,Siyuan Chen,Omkar Salunkhe,Ebru Turanoglu Bekar,Johan Stahre,Anders Skoogh*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于语言模型的工作流，用于从自然语言任务描述生成、验证并迭代修正ABB RAPID机器人程序。通过双流检索增强生成（RAG）管道将代码生成锚定在技术文档和生产模板中，并利用自定义Model Context Protocol（MCP）服务器连接ABB RobotStudio实现自动代码上传、仿真执行和诊断反馈。评估结合30条检索基准、代码生成检查和RobotStudio拾取-放置仿真案例，仿真回路暴露了静态和语义检查无法捕获的执行失败。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 柔性制造要求工业机器人在产品变体变化时能够快速重新编程，而传统手工编程效率低下。未经过接地（ungrounded）的语言模型在生成机器人代码时会产生大量领域特定错误，因此需要一种将代码生成与可验证技术文档、可执行仿真反馈相结合的自动化工作流。

Method: 提出语言模型驱动的RAPID程序生成工作流，包含三个核心组件：(1) 双流RAG管道，从技术文档和生产模板中检索接地信息以指导代码生成；(2) 自定义MCP服务器，将语言模型客户端直接连接到ABB RobotStudio，实现代码自动上传、仿真执行和诊断信息回传；(3) 迭代修正循环，利用仿真反馈（如吸盘释放高度错误、不可达放置目标、配置相关恢复运动）持续改进生成的程序。

Result: 在30条检索基准、代码生成检查和RobotStudio拾取-放置制造单元的仿真案例上进行了综合评估。仿真回路成功暴露了静态和语义检查无法捕获的执行失败类型，表明RAG和MCP能够将接地代码生成与工业机器人仿真软件的可执行反馈连接起来，但减少了而非完全消除了专家设置和最终监督的需求。

Conclusion: RAG与MCP的组合可以有效提升基于语言模型的工业机器人编程的可靠性和实用性，仿真反馈回路对于捕获真实执行层面的错误不可或缺，但目前仍需要一定程度的专家监督，尚不能实现完全自动化。

Abstract: Flexible manufacturing requires industrial robots to be reprogrammed rapidly as product variants change. This paper presents a language-model-based workflow that generates, validates, and iteratively corrects ABB RAPID robot programs from natural language task descriptions. A dual-stream retrieval-augmented generation (RAG) pipeline grounds code generation in verified technical documentation and production templates, reducing domain-specific errors produced by ungrounded language models. A custom Model Context Protocol (MCP) server connects the language-model client directly to ABB RobotStudio for automated code upload, simulation execution, and diagnostic feedback. The evaluation combines a 30-query retrieval benchmark, scoped code-generation checks, and RobotStudio case studies in a simulated pickand- place manufacturing cell. The simulation loop exposes execution failures that static and semantic checks alone cannot catch, including suction release-height errors, unreachable placement targets, and configuration-dependent recovery motions. The results show how RAG and MCP can connect grounded code generation with executable feedback from industrial robot simulation software, while reducing but not eliminating expert setup and final supervision.

</details>


### [87] [Composable Trust Infrastructure for Manufacturing Knowledge Graphs: Cross-System Provenance, Temporal Reasoning, and Decision Traceability](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.21418)
*Grama Chethan*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种可组合的信任基础设施，将SHACL验证、PROV-O溯源、领域感知双时态版本控制和图原生决策对象四种能力整合到统一的RDF架构中，通过共享实体URI、摄取活动标识符和时间相关性键实现组合，实验证明四种能力缺一不可，并发现四种涌现的三方属性和一种不可约的四方向属性（全链路可审计性，31ms执行）。在整合11个工业数据源、89类ISA-95对齐本体、8,743条三元组的测试平台上进行了验证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 工业知识图谱在整合异构工业系统数据时面临信任赤字：消费者无法判断查询数据是否有效、决策时数据是否有效、数据来源何处、以及数据如何被使用。单一能力无法解决这一多维信任问题，需要探索多种信任能力的组合效应。

Method: 提出四种信任能力（SHACL验证、PROV-O溯源、域感知双时态版本控制、图原生决策对象）通过共享实体URI、摄取活动标识符和时间相关性键在统一RDF架构中组合。采用消融实验验证每种能力的负载贡献，并通过高阶组合分析揭示涌现的信任属性。

Result: 消融实验表明移除任一能力恰好导致六个组合查询中的三个失败，证明四种能力同等关键。高阶分析发现四种涌现的三方属性和一种不可约的四方向属性（全链路可审计性，31ms执行）。在整合11个工业数据源、89类ISA-95本体、8,743条三元组、81条owl:sameAs边（横跨5个命名图）的测试平台上得到验证。

Conclusion: 四种信任能力通过共享相关性标识符的组合产生超越单一能力的涌现信任属性，所有能力均为基础负载，且存在一种不可约的四方向组合属性（全链路可审计性），说明了信任基础设施组合设计的必要性。验证使用结构现实但模拟的仿真数据，数据结构和跨系统链接模式具有工业代表性。

Abstract: Manufacturing knowledge graphs that integrate data from heterogeneous industrial systems face a trust deficit: consumers cannot determine whether queried data is valid, whether it was valid when a decision was made, where it originated, or how it was acted upon. We argue that four trust capabilities -- SHACL validation, PROV-O provenance, domain-aware bi-temporal versioning, and graph-native decision objects -- compose through shared correlation identifiers to produce emergent trust properties that no single capability delivers alone. We present a composable trust infrastructure that integrates these four capabilities into a unified RDF architecture. Capabilities compose through shared entity URIs, ingestion activity identifiers, and temporal correlation keys, enabling compound queries spanning all four dimensions. An experimental ablation confirms that removing any single capability causes exactly three of six composition queries to fail, demonstrating that all four are equally load-bearing. Analysis of higher-order compositions reveals four emergent three-way properties and one irreducible four-way property (full-chain auditability, 31ms execution). The infrastructure is validated on a testbed integrating eleven industrial sources -- OPC UA, TIA Portal, eClass, AAS, ISA-95, ISA-18.2, SAP S/4HANA, Teamcenter, Opcenter EX, Insights Hub, and SCM -- under an 89-class ISA-95-aligned ontology. The unified graph contains 8,743 triples across five named graphs, stitched by 81 owl:sameAs identity edges. Evaluation uses simulated but structurally realistic data from purpose-built emulators; data structures and cross-system linkage patterns are representative of real industrial installations.

</details>


### [88] [Agentic AI for Safety-critical Multi-drone Systems: Challenges and Opportunities](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.21444)
*Timothy Merritt,Alejandro Jarabo-Peñas,Juan Bravo-Arrabal,Maria-Theresa Bahodi,Anders Lyhne Christensen*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文是一篇position paper，基于NAMUR和PERSIST两个项目，主张将多无人机系统中的智能体AI视为社会技术设计问题，强调不仅需提升自主性能，更需关注操作者理解、信任和治理自动化能力，并提出以人为中心、参与式和迭代的研究方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多无人机系统在搜救和关键基础设施监控等安全关键任务中日益重要，但实际部署受限于自动化整合进专业工作的难度，操作者需要在不确定、时间压力和问责制条件下理解、信任和治理自动化。现有研究过度聚焦算法自主性能，忽视了接口、监督机制和评估实践等社会技术层面。

Method: 通过综合两个在研项目的实践经验进行论证：NAMUR项目探索LLM支持的机器人在搜救与消防场景中的控制，PERSIST项目探索关键基础设施站点持续无人机监控与安全。提出以人为中心、参与式和迭代的研究范式，包括识别利益相关者需求、通过连续原型塑造智能体能力、产出可迁移的概念验证系统和评估策略。

Result: 提出了将智能体AI视为社会技术设计问题的框架，论证明晰了接口、监督机制和评估实践与算法同等重要，并勾勒出面向安全关键场景的可迁移设计和评估方法。

Conclusion: 应把智能体AI的发展定位为以人为本、参与式和迭代的社会技术过程，而非单纯的算法工程问题，以促进多无人机系统在安全关键领域中的实际采用。

Abstract: Multi-drone systems are increasingly positioned for safety-critical missions such as search and rescue (SAR) and critical infrastructure monitoring. Yet, real-world adoption remains constrained not only by autonomy performance, but by the difficulty of integrating agentic behavior into professional work: operators must understand, trust, and govern automation under uncertainty, time pressure, and accountability. This position paper synthesizes the ambitions and lessons from two ongoing efforts: NAMUR, which explores LLM-supported robot control in SAR and firefighting contexts, and PERSIST, which explores persistent drone operations for monitoring and security at critical infrastructure sites. We argue that agentic AI should be approached as a socio-technical design problem, where interfaces, oversight mechanisms, and evaluation practices are as critical as algorithms. We outline a human-centered, participatory, and iterative research approach aimed at uncovering stakeholder needs, shaping agent capabilities through successive prototypes, and producing transferable proof-of-concept systems and evaluation strategies for other safety-critical contexts.

</details>


### [89] [Software Frameworks for Explainable AI in Time Series Classification: A Systematic Review](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.21449)
*Louis Peter,Nils Gumpfer,Jana Fischer,Christin Seifert,Jennifer Hannig*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文是首个针对时间序列分类（TSC）中可解释人工智能（XAI）软件框架的系统性综述，比较了六个支持时间序列的XAI框架，涵盖支持的XAI方法、评估指标、可用性、基准测试支持和可复现性等多个维度，并首次分析了频域支持情况。研究发现当前框架存在显著局限：仅一个方法支持频域解释、仅两个评估指标专为时间序列设计、相同XAI方法在不同框架中产生显著不同的解释。基于这些发现，作者讨论了开放挑战并指出了未来研究方向。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 时间序列分类在决策关键场景中广泛应用，XAI方法的透明性和可信度需求日益迫切，但当前XAI用于时间序列的研究零散，缺乏对可用软件框架、评估实践和实际局限的系统性理解。以往研究多聚焦单一解释方法，跨框架一致性、时间序列特定评估和可复现性被忽视。

Method: 作者对现有用于TSC中解释生成与评估的软件框架进行了系统性调研，沿多个维度进行对比分析，包括支持的XAI方法、评估指标、可用性、基准测试支持和可复现性。这是首个具有实现对比和时间序列特定框架综述，并包含频域支持分析。

Result: 识别出六个明确支持时间序列的框架，并揭示了共同局限：仅一种方法支持频域解释（尽管频域在时间序列中具有相关性）；仅两种评估指标是专门为时间序列开发的；相同的XAI方法在不同框架中会产生实质性不同的解释结果。

Conclusion: 当前TSC的XAI框架存在碎片化和不一致性问题，未来需要统一的、时间序列特定的XAI框架，以实现忠实、可复现且时间序列感知的解释。作者基于研究发现讨论了开放挑战并概述了未来研究方向。

Abstract: Time series arise in a wide range of application domains and are analyzed using machine learning in decision-critical settings. Time series classification (TSC) is one of the most widely studied and relevant tasks. In this context, ensuring the transparency and trustworthiness of TSC models has become an important requirement, motivating the use of explainable artificial intelligence (XAI) methods. Despite growing interest, research on XAI for TSC remains fragmented, and a systematic understanding of the available software frameworks for explanation generation, their evaluation practices, and practical limitations is still lacking. Prior work largely focused on individual explanation methods, while cross-framework consistency, time-series-specific evaluation, and reproducibility have received little attention. In this survey, we analyze existing software frameworks for explanation generation and evaluation in TSC. We compare them along multiple dimensions, including supported XAI methods, evaluation metrics, usability, benchmarking support, and reproducibility, providing the first time-series-specific survey of frameworks with implementation comparisons and an analysis of frequency-domain support. We identify six frameworks that explicitly support time series and reveal common limitations: only one method supports frequency-domain explanations despite their relevance; only two evaluation metrics have been developed specifically for time series; and identical XAI methods can yield substantially different explanations across frameworks. Based on these findings, we discuss open challenges and outline directions for future research, highlighting the need for unified, time-series-specific XAI frameworks that enable faithful, reproducible, and time-series-aware explanations.

</details>


### [90] [Enhanced Artificial Neural Networks Using QHAdamW in Air Quality Forecasting](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.21463)
*Mary Joy Daniel Vinas*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种结合QHAdamW优化器的前馈神经网络模型，用于菲律宾马尼拉的空气质量指数（AQI）预测，分别预测PM2.5和PM10浓度。该模型是菲律宾目前唯一的AQI预测模型，通过在Adam优化器基础上融合Quasi-Hyperbolic Momentum和decoupled weight decay，解决了收敛性、泛化性和预测性能问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的Adam优化器在训练人工神经网络时存在收敛性、泛化性和预测性能方面的缺陷，需要一种改进的优化器来提升AQI预测模型的精度和稳定性。

Method: 将QHAdam（Quasi-Hyperbolic Momentum）和AdamW（带解耦权重衰减的Adam）两种扩展优化器融合，构建QHAdamW优化器，并应用于前馈神经网络（ANN）框架中，用于AQI预测。通过超参数调优确定最优学习率，并使用七种评估指标进行性能对比。

Result: 超参数调优显示0.01和0.001是最有效的泛化性能最优值；七种评估指标的对比分析显示误差值范围更低，回归系数约等于1，模型精度显著提升；模型收敛性能良好，训练和验证损失值较低。

Conclusion: QHAdamW优化器能有效提升ANN模型的AQI预测精度和收敛性能，可用于PM2.5和PM10的实时预测，为DENR-EMB实施综合空气质量管理提供决策支持。

Abstract: The study employed an Artificial Neural Network in combination with the optimized Adaptive Moment Estimation (Adam) algorithm, currently the only AQI forecasting model available in the Philippines. The modified QHAdamW - Quasi-Hyperbolic Momentum (QHAdam) and Adam with decoupled weight decay (AdamW) were both extensions of the Adam optimizer, and both offer unique advantages for training ANN. The proposed QHAdamW optimizer addresses the issues on convergence, generalization, and forecasting performance of Adam. Hyperparameter tuning results revealed that 0.01 and 0.001 were the most effective optimal values for the generalization performance of QHAdamW. The comparative analysis results using seven evaluation metrics revealed that the error value range is lower, and the regression coefficient, having a value approximately equal to 1, improved the model accuracy performance. Likewise, the model converges to a satisfactory level of performance with the convergence performance results of lower loss values as obtained from training and validation losses. Based on data from a real-time air quality tracking station in Manila, a feed-forward neural network is used to predict the AQI of PM2.5 and PM10 separately. This model can be used to forecast Particulate Matter (PM), to help the Department of Environment and Natural Resources-Environmental Monitoring Bureau (DENR-EMB) implement a comprehensive air quality management.

</details>


### [91] [Quantifying geographic domain shift to decouple the geospatial transferability of human mobility flow generation models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.21567)
*Zhiyong Zhou,Song Gao,Qianheng Zhang,Feng Zhang,Zhenhong Du*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文系统研究了四种人类移动性生成模型在地理空间上的可迁移性，基于美国2265个县的通勤流大规模基准数据集，引入地理域偏移概念并提出了互信息和空间偏移两个量化指标，通过线性混合效应回归揭示了地理域偏移与模型可迁移性之间的显著关联。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 人类移动性是理解城市系统中社会、经济与环境动态的重要代理指标。地理空间可迁移性（模型在新地点或未见区域的泛化能力）是评估不同人类移动性生成模型的关键维度，然而现有研究很少深入探究可迁移性的内在特征及其影响因素。

Method: 研究采用以下方法：（1）使用包含美国2265个县的人口普查区级通勤流的大规模基准数据集；（2）受机器学习中域自适应理论启发，引入"地理域偏移"概念描述源区域与目标区域在地理特征分布和空间结构上的内在差异；（3）提出互信息与空间偏移两个指标量化地理域偏移；（4）使用线性混合效应回归分析地理域偏移与模型可迁移性之间的关联。

Result: 研究发现迁移性能在不同区域间存在显著的空间异质性和不对称性；信息偏移和空间偏移均具有统计显著的、互补的解释力。这表明地理空间可迁移性不仅取决于模型设计，还取决于源与目标区域间内在的地理差异。

Conclusion: 本研究为评估和改进人类移动性生成模型的地理空间可迁移性提供了新的方法论框架，支持跨区域更稳健、更公平的人类移动性数据合成，并为GeoAI模型开发中的空间可迁移性提供了重要见解。

Abstract: Human mobility serves as an essential proxy for understanding social, economic, and environmental dynamics in urban systems. Geospatial transferability, which measures a model's capability in a new location or unseen region, is a critical dimension for comparing different human mobility generation models. However, few studies have studied the intrinsic characteristics of geospatial transferability. To this end, this study systematically investigates the geospatial transferability of four representative human mobility generation models using a large-scale benchmark dataset of census tract level commuting flows across 2265 counties in the United States. Inspired by the domain adaptation theory in machine learning, we introduce geographic domain shift to describe the intrinsic differences in geographic feature distributions and spatial structures between source and target regions, which may jointly affect model transferability. Moreover, we propose two metrics, mutual information and spatial shift, to quantify the geographic domain shift. To examine their associations with model transferability, we employ linear mixed-effects regression to analyze the associations between geographic domain shifts and transferability. Our results reveal substantial spatial heterogeneity and asymmetry in transfer performance across regions. Both information shift and spatial shift exhibit statistically significant and complementary explanatory power. This indicates that geospatial transferability depends not only on model design but also on intrinsic geographic differences. These findings provide a novel methodological framework for evaluating and improving the geospatial transferability of human mobility generation models and support more robust and fair human mobility data synthesis across diverse regions. It also offers insights on spatial transferability for GeoAI model development.

</details>


### [92] [VisAdj: Learning Adjacency Matrices from Node-Link Images](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.21825)
*Jiahao Xie,Guangmo Tong*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出VisAdj框架，用于从节点-链接图像中学习邻接矩阵。该框架引入了注意力稀疏邻居采样器自适应选择高召回候选节点对，并使用线图Transformer将候选边作为token进行联合边推理，显式建模相邻边之间的依赖关系。在合成图、道路网络和血管图像上的实验表明，VisAdj显著优于现有基线方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有方法通常依赖固定的KNN启发式策略进行候选边选择，无法捕捉边之间的依赖关系，导致从节点-链接图像中恢复结构化图信息的效果受限。

Method: VisAdj框架包含两个核心组件：(1) 注意力稀疏邻居采样器，用于自适应地选择高召回率的候选节点对；(2) 线图Transformer，将候选边视为token，通过联合边推理显式建模相邻边之间的依赖关系。

Result: 在合成图、道路网络和血管图像三个数据集上的广泛实验表明，VisAdj在各项指标上均稳定优于现有基线方法，且优势明显。

Conclusion: 拓扑感知的邻接矩阵预测是可行的，通过自适应候选采样和边依赖建模可以显著提升从视觉观察中恢复图结构信息的准确性。

Abstract: Learning adjacency matrices from node-link images is a fundamental problem for recovering structured graph information from visual observations. Existing methods typically rely on fixed KNN-based heuristics for candidate edge selection and fail to capture dependencies among edges. To overcome these limitations, we propose VisAdj, a new framework for topology-aware adjacency prediction. VisAdj introduces an attention-sparse neighbor sampler to adaptively select a high-recall set of candidate node pairs and performs joint edge inference using a line-graph transformer that treats candidate edges as tokens and explicitly models dependencies among incident edges. Extensive experiments on synthetic graphs, road networks, and vessel images demonstrate that VisAdj consistently outperforms existing baselines by clear margins.

</details>


### [93] [GameXpert-Bench: How Far Are Coding Agents from Expert Game Development?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.21833)
*Kun Chen,Haorong Hong,Peizhong Gao,Jianfeng Lin,Tongxu Luo,Yuxuan Xie,Chenxu Liu,Jieling He,Zhongyuan Liu,Zeno Zeng*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出GameXpert-Bench，一个覆盖游戏开发全生命周期的基准测试套件，包含三个互补的track：GameGen（初始游戏生成）、GameFix（缺陷诊断与修复）、GameOpt（多轮累积优化），共含97个生成任务、100个修复任务和17个优化链。评测发现当前编码代理擅长生成可玩的游戏基础和实现明确需求，但在发现缺陷、验证运行时行为和跨变更保持功能方面仍存在不足。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基准通常只评估LLM游戏开发能力的最终产物或单一开发阶段，缺乏对完整开发生命周期（从生成到修复到优化）的系统评估。作者通过分析完整的人-代理开发轨迹，识别出游戏开发中三个关键阶段，并据此设计覆盖全生命周期的评测基准。

Method: 首先分析完整的人类-编码代理开发轨迹，提取出游戏开发生命周期的三个阶段：初始游戏生成、缺陷诊断与修复、多轮优化。然后构建GameXpert-Bench，将其分别实现为三个track：GameGen（从空工作区的单一请求创建完整游戏）、GameFix（50个经人工验证的游戏关卡中注入19-27个bug，共100个修复任务）、GameOpt（基于真实用户-代理开发轨迹生成的17条请求链，每条6轮共102个请求）。每个track采用不同的评估方式：实时游戏交互、确定性行为测试、或带回归检查的最终产品标准。

Result: 基准包含97个跨11种游戏类型的生成任务、100个修复任务（50个关卡×19-27个注入bug）和17条优化链（6轮/102个请求）。评估发现当前代理在生成可玩游戏基础和实现明确需求方面较可靠，但在缺陷发现、运行时行为验证和跨变更保持功能方面明显不足。

Conclusion: GameXpert-Bench揭示了当前LLM编码代理在游戏开发中的能力边界：基础生成能力已较成熟，但全生命周期的可靠性仍需提升，特别是在自主发现缺陷、验证运行时行为和保持功能一致性方面，为未来研究指明了方向。

Abstract: Recent large language models (LLMs) can operate as coding agents that build complete games from natural language requests. Game development is especially demanding because program logic, visual and audio content, interfaces, interaction and playability must function together in one executable artifact. Measuring this capability therefore requires evaluation of both game product and the development process. Existing benchmarks often assess the game development capabilities of LLMs by evaluating the final artifact or an isolated development stage. Our analysis of complete human-agent development trajectories identifies three stages that together span the lifecycle of game development with a coding agent: initial game generation, bug diagnosis and repair, and optimization over multiple turns. Therefore, we introduce GameXpert-Bench, which operationalizes the three lifecycle stages as three complementary benchmark tracks. GameGen evaluates complete game creation from a single request in an empty workspace. GameFix evaluates diagnosis and repair when defects are reported or left for the agent to discover. GameOpt evaluates cumulative optimization through request chains seeded by real development trajectories between users and agents. We evaluate each track using live game interaction, deterministic behavioral tests, or final product criteria with regression checks. The suite contains 97 generation tasks across 11 genres; 100 repair tasks from 50 game levels verified by humans, each with 19-27 injected bugs; and 17 optimization chains with six turns and 102 requests. Across the three tracks, current agents are more reliable at producing playable foundations and implementing explicit requirements than at discovering defects, verifying runtime behavior, and preserving functionality across changes.

</details>


### [94] [LLM4LLM: Bridging Kernel Benchmarks and Real Deployment via Closed-Loop Agentic Optimization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.21836)
*Hui Zeng,Pengfei Yang,Yanxin Chen,Fusong Ju,Xinran Wei*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出LLM4LLM框架，针对大语言模型推理中的kernel优化问题，识别出独立kernel基准测试与真实部署之间的"benchmark-to-deployment gap"，并引入部署感知的闭环优化框架，在真实推理工作负载中实现端到端加速。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有大语言模型在底层代码和kernel优化方面表现越来越强，但孤立的kernel基准测试只能作为部署行为的近似代理。候选kernel在独立测试环境中表现正确且快速，但集成到真实推理工作负载后可能表现出不同的性能、安全性或阶段行为，存在明显的"benchmark-to-deployment gap"。

Method: LLM4LLM是一个部署感知的闭环优化框架，其流程包括：(1) 从目标推理脚本出发；(2) 提取阶段感知的优化任务；(3) 使用经验引导的episodic agent进行搜索；(4) 通过模型内验证接受补丁。

Result: 在A100和H100 GPU上的十个语言模型推理工作负载中，LLM4LLM对每个评估模型都改善了端到端延迟，在A100/H100上分别实现3.91倍和6.98倍的几何平均加速；作为kernel级支持证据，在KernelBench Level 2上也获得了最高2.745倍的几何平均加速。

Conclusion: LLM4LLM验证了部署感知的闭环优化框架能够有效弥合基准测试与真实部署之间的差距，在端到端推理延迟上取得显著加速，同时kernel级优化效果也得到验证。

Abstract: Large language models have become increasingly capable agents for low-level code and kernel optimization, but isolated kernel benchmarks provide only a proxy for the deployment behavior that matters in language-model inference. We identify a benchmark-to-deployment gap: candidate kernels that appear correct and fast in standalone harnesses can exhibit different performance, safety, or phase behavior after integration into a real inference workload. We introduce LLM4LLM, a deployment-aware closed-loop optimization framework that starts from a target inference script, extracts phase-aware optimization tasks, searches with an experience-guided episodic agent, and accepts patches through in-model validation. Across ten language-model inference workloads on A100 and H100 GPUs, LLM4LLM improves end-to-end latency for every evaluated model, achieving 3.91$\times$/6.98$\times$ geometric-mean speedups on A100/H100; as supporting kernel-level evidence, it also attains up to 2.745$\times$ GeoMean speedup on KernelBench Level 2.

</details>


### [95] [AI Watchdog: Agent Interfaces for Detecting and Defending Against Manipulative Dark Patterns in AI Conversations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.21841)
*Rachel Poonsiriwong,Chayapatr,Archiwaranguprok,Constanze Albrecht,Monchai Lertsutthiwong,Pattie Maes,Pat Pataranutaporn*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出AI Watchdog，一个监控实时对话、检测五种暗黑模式（谄媚、品牌偏见、拟人化、偷偷摸摸和有害生成）的浏览器代理界面。通过预注册的五条件被试间实验（N=150）发现，参与者很少主动标记操控性对话轮次，但"即时警告+无认知强制"是唯一显著降低用户对AI引导建议服从率的干预措施，将服从率从71.7%降至53.7%（降低18个百分点）。研究还揭示了明确的暗黑模式识别与行为抵抗可能是两个独立的结果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 会话式AI日益影响重大决策，但用户缺乏识别和抵抗操控的支持。现有研究尚未系统性地提供实时防御工具来帮助用户识别对话中的暗黑模式。

Method: 构建AI Watchdog浏览器代理界面，使用开放权重的逐轮分类器检测五类暗黑模式；通过预注册的五条件被试间实验（N=150）比较无干预控制组与四种配置（预置诱导vs即时警告；有无认知强制）的效果。

Result: 参与者各条件下都很少标记操控性轮次；任务后意识在各组间无显著差异；仅"即时警告无认知强制"显著降低服从率（71.7%→53.7%，降18个百分点）；探索性分析显示较低的错误信息易感性关联更高标记率但不关联更低服从率，较高的AI信任关联更高服从率和更低报告意识。

Conclusion: 明确识别对话暗黑模式与行为抵抗AI引导可能是独立的结果变量，提示应进一步研究低摩擦、及时的防御性界面设计。

Abstract: Conversational AI increasingly shapes consequential decisions, yet users have limited support for recognizing and resisting manipulation. We present AI Watchdog, a browser-based agent interface that monitors live conversations, detects five dark-pattern categories, including sycophancy, brand bias, anthropomorphization, sneaking, and harmful generation, and alerts users when they occur. Its open-weight turn-level classifier supports independent deployment and a path toward local inference, preserving user privacy while remaining separate from the conversational AI. We evaluated AI Watchdog in a preregistered, five-condition between-subjects experiment (N = 150) comparing a no-intervention control with four configurations varying nudge timing (prebunking vs. just-in-time) and engagement mode (without vs. with cognitive forcing). Results show that participants rarely flagged manipulative turns across all conditions, and post-task awareness did not differ significantly across groups. However, just-in-time warnings without cognitive forcing were the only intervention to significantly reduce compliance with AI-steered recommendations containing dark patterns, lowering compliance from 71.7% to 53.7%, an 18 percentage-point reduction. Exploratory analyses further showed that lower misinformation susceptibility was associated with greater flagging but not lower compliance, while higher AI trust was associated with greater compliance and lower reported awareness. Together, these findings suggest that explicit recognition of conversational dark patterns and behavioral resistance to AI steering may be distinct outcomes, motivating further investigation of timely, low-friction defensive interfaces.

</details>


### [96] [MemGuard: Persisting Verifier Signals for LLM-Agent Memory Governance](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.21867)
*Haoyu Wang,Guangyuan Dong,He Liang,Zijing Zhang,Jiachen Luo,Chuang Liu,Chao Xue,Hao Tang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: MemGuard 将验证器输出从一次性过滤器转变为持久化的生命周期元数据，将多准则的评分-令牌验证转换为奖励、置信度、标签和不确定性描述符，并附加到每个候选记忆条目上，在检索、冲突消解、摘要和归档阶段复用，以解决 LLM 智能体长期任务流中记忆的不可靠录入和记忆漂移两大问题。在 Terminal-Bench 2.0、SWE-Bench Verified、WebArena 和 Mind2Web 四个基准上，跨四个骨干模型，MemGuard 在所有 16 个设置中均取得最优成功率指标和最低平均步数。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM 智能体正从单次提示使用转向长任务流，可复用记忆成为终端、软件工程和 Web 任务的核心能力。然而记忆仅在跨数百次交互中保持可靠时才有效，实践中存在两大失效模式：一是不可靠录入（失败轨迹、偶然成功和误导性观察因表面相关而进入记忆，进而误导后续决策）；二是记忆漂移（长期运行的记忆库积累重复、过时和冲突的记录，仅靠检索无法修复）。

Method: MemGuard 的核心创新在于将验证器输出视为持久化的生命周期元数据而非一次性过滤器。它将多准则的评分-令牌验证转化为奖励、置信度、标签和不确定性四类描述符，在每条候选记忆激活前附加这些描述符，并在检索、冲突消解、摘要和归档各个阶段反复利用这些元数据来维护记忆的可靠性。

Result: 在 Terminal-Bench 2.0、SWE-Bench Verified、WebArena 和 Mind2Web 四个基准上，跨四种骨干模型，与四个记忆基线及一个仅验证器对照组（匹配运行时间预算）比较，五个随机种子的平均值显示 MemGuard 在所有 16 个骨干-基准组合中均取得最佳成功率指标和最低平均步数。相比最强的先前基线 ReasoningBank，最大提升为 WebArena 上 7.9 个成功率点、Mind2Web 上 5.6 个步骤成功率点、终端与软件工程基准上 2.4-3.5 个点。

Conclusion: MemGuard 通过将验证器输出作为持久化生命周期元数据来管理记忆，有效解决了 LLM 智能体长任务流中的不可靠录入和记忆漂移问题，在多个基准和骨干模型上均一致优于现有记忆基线方法，验证了该方法的普适性和有效性。

Abstract: LLM agents are moving from single-prompt use to long task streams in which reusable memory becomes a core capability for terminal, software-engineering, and web tasks. Such memory is useful only when stored experience remains reliable across hundreds of interactions, but two failure modes break that assumption in practice. The first is unreliable admission: failed trajectories,accidental successes, and misleading observations enter memory because they appear relevant, then mislead later decisions. The second is memory drift: long-running banks accumulate duplicate, stale, and conflicting records that retrieval alone cannot repair. MemGuard's key distinction is to treat verifier output not as a one-shot filter, but as persistent lifecycle metadata. It converts multi-criteria score-token verification into reward, confidence, label, and uncertainty descriptors that are attached to every candidate before activation and reused during retrieval, conflict resolution, summarization, and archival. We evaluate MemGuard on Terminal-Bench 2.0, SWE-Bench Verified, WebArena, and Mind2Web across four backbones, comparing against four memory baselines plus a verifier-only control under matched runtime budgets. Averaged over five seeds, MemGuard achieves the best success metric and lowest average steps in all 16 backbone-benchmark settings, improving over ReasoningBank, the strongest prior baseline among the memory methods we evaluate, with a largest gain of 7.9 success-rate points on WebArena, 5.6 step-success-rate points on Mind2Web, and 2.4-3.5 points on terminal and software-engineering benchmarks. Code is available at https://github.com/whyyyyy123/MemGuard.

</details>


### [97] [HiMA-MDD: A Hierarchical Multi-Agent Harness for Interpretable Multimodal Depression Detection in Clinical Interviews](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.21868)
*Ao Chen,Xiaojiang Peng*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出HiMA-MDD，一个分层多智能体框架，用于从多模态临床访谈中评估抑郁（PHQ-8量表）。该框架将评估层级与三个智能体层对齐：L1识别QA与量表条目关系并路由证据，L2为症状组指派因子专家负责暂定条目得分，L3审计完整暂定画像并进行一轮修订。在E-DAIC数据集上超越SOTA方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有LLM系统要么整体性处理访谈，要么在通用智能体角色间分配工作，均缺乏显式的编排机制来协调跨层级的证据访问、条目得分权威、受限反馈和状态记录。抑郁评估本质上是分层级的：证据稀疏且依赖上下文、多个Q&A共同支撑症状判断、最终评估依赖完整症状画像的一致性。

Method: HiMA-MDD采用三层分层多智能体架构：(1) 非智能体预处理构建保留上下文的QA单元；(2) Layer 1识别候选QA与条目间关系并做有界条目导向的证据路由；(3) Layer 2将症状组分配给操作性因子专家，每位专家负责一个暂定条目得分；(4) Layer 3审计完整暂定画像，最多请求一轮针对性修订，重建已验证的PHQ-8画像。该设计自然产生分层证据轨迹（Hierarchical Evidence Trace），保留所有中间证据、判断和修订以供审计。最终条目得分确定性地产出总分和筛查决策。以Qwen2.5-72B-Instruct作为骨干。

Result: 在E-DAIC数据集上的实验表明，HiMA-MDD以Qwen2.5-72B-Instruct为骨干，性能优于所比较的最先进方法。

Conclusion: 分层多智能体编排机制能有效对齐临床抑郁评估的层次结构，通过显式的证据路由、因子专家分权和受限修订审计，既提升了评估性能，又保留了完整可审计的证据轨迹。

Abstract: Depression assessment from multimodal clinical interviews requires integrating dispersed evidence from multiple symptoms into a coherent PHQ-8 profile. This process is hierarchical: relevant evidence is often sparse and context-dependent within local question-answer exchanges, multiple exchanges jointly support symptom-level judgments, and the final assessment depends on the coherence of the complete symptom profile. Existing LLM systems either process interviews holistically or distribute work across generic agent roles; neither design necessarily provides an explicit orchestration mechanism that coordinates evidence access, item-score authority, bounded feedback, and state recording across these levels. To address this gap, we introduce HiMA-MDD, a hierarchical multi-agent harness that aligns this assessment hierarchy with three agent layers. After non-agentic preprocessing constructs context-preserving multimodal QA units, Layer 1 identifies candidate QA-to-item relations and supports bounded item-grounded evidence routing. Layer 2 assigns symptom groups to operational factor specialists, with one specialist responsible for each provisional item score. Layer 3 audits the complete provisional profile, requests at most one round of targeted revision, and reconstructs the verified PHQ-8 profile. This layered design naturally yields a Hierarchical Evidence Trace, preserves all intermediate evidence, judgments, and revisions for auditability. The final item scores then deterministically produce the total score and screening decision. Using Qwen2.5-72B-Instruct as the harness backbone, our experiments on E-DAIC demonstrate that HiMA-MDD outperforms the compared state-of-the-art methods.

</details>


### [98] [Training Needs Trustworthy Worlds: Verified Synthetic Web Environments for Agent Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.21898)
*Chenghao Zhang,Canran Xiao,SaiSai Hu,Dan Roth*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种可执行、可审计、且基于后端状态的可验证合成Web环境构建框架，通过结构化脚手架表示页面、导航链接、数据库记录和任务约束，并在策略训练前修复结构、语义、一致性和可行性缺陷。实验表明该方法将可行任务率从48.6%提升至94.8%，产生了更强的PPO策略，并提升了向WebArena、WebShop和MiniWoB++的迁移效果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有Web代理训练依赖合成环境，但这些环境往往只在外观上看似合理，实际存在断裂链接、不一致状态或不可行任务，导致训练信号不可靠，无法为代理学习提供可信的监督。

Method: 将每个生成的网站表示为结构化脚手架（页面、导航链接、数据库记录、状态变更标记和任务约束），训练前验证并修复结构、语义、一致性和可行性缺陷；交互时UI转换确定性执行，持久后端更新仅通过验证的状态变更标记调用，从而根据已验证的任务进度谓词编译密集奖励。

Result: 在500个涵盖六个领域的合成环境中，任务阻塞缺陷减少，可行任务率从48.6%提高到94.8%；产生更强的PPO策略，且不需LLM推理即可改善对WebArena、WebShop和MiniWoB++的迁移。

Conclusion: 验证过的合成环境可作为紧凑Web代理的可扩展且可靠训练基座，将合成Web代理学习从表面合理性转向可执行、状态可追溯的监督。

Abstract: Web agents promise to automate complex digital workflows, but their training remains limited by synthetic environments that look plausible while hiding broken links, inconsistent states, or infeasible tasks. We address the gap between scalable environment generation and trustworthy agent learning by constructing synthetic web environments that are executable, auditable, and grounded in backend state. Our framework represents each generated website as a structured scaffold of pages, navigation links, database records, state-change markers, and task constraints, then verifies and repairs structural, semantic, consistency, and feasibility defects before policy training. During interaction, ordinary UI transitions are executed deterministically, while persistent backend updates are invoked only through validated state-change markers, enabling dense rewards compiled from verified task-progress predicates. Across 500 synthetic environments spanning six domains, our method reduces task-blocking defects and improves feasible-task rate from 48.6% to 94.8%, while producing stronger PPO policies and improving transfer to WebArena, WebShop, and MiniWoB++ without LLM calls at evaluation time. These results show that verified synthetic environments can serve as a scalable and reliable training substrate for compact web agents, shifting synthetic webagent learning from surface-level plausibility toward executable, state-grounded supervision.

</details>


### [99] [ESCRAG-R1: Retrieval-Augmented Reinforcement Learning for Emotional Support Conversation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.21925)
*Weichu Liu,Yuxuan Hu,Yirong Sun,Ningning Mao,Ziyun Zhang,Jian Chen,Mingyang Xu,Qishan Zhong,Chengming Li*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出ESCRAG-R1，一种将检索增强的心理引导整合进GRPO强化学习框架的统一情感支持对话系统，通过ESC-Preference数据集提供可靠的奖励信号，实现了专业指导与自然共情的无缝融合，显著优于现有基线方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有情感支持对话系统难以同时实现结构化、阶段感知的推理与共情-专业能力的自然对齐，往往导致临床策略与通用安慰的人工拼接，缺乏真正的融合效果。

Method: ESCRAG-R1将检索式心理引导融入GRPO强化学习循环中，检索将外部知识转化为稳健的学习信号，在生成前激发显式内部推理并重塑模型内部策略；同时构建基于客户-咨询师-评委评估框架的ESC-Preference高质量数据集，提供精准且共情感知的奖励信号以支撑可靠监督。

Result: 大量实验表明ESCRAG-R1在缓解人工拼接问题、实现专业指导与共情表达自然融合方面显著优于现有基线方法。

Conclusion: 检索增强与GRPO结合的统一框架能够有效解决情感支持对话中专业性与共情性难以协调的问题，为后续研究提供了开源代码和数据集。

Abstract: Emotional Support Conversation (ESC) systems aim to provide holistic support by balancing professional therapeutic competence with natural empathy. However, existing methods struggle to simultaneously achieve structured, stage-aware reasoning and seamless empathy-expertise alignment, often resulting in an artificial splicing of clinical strategies and generic reassurance. To overcome these limitations, we propose ESCRAG-R1, a unified framework that integrates retrieval-based psychological guidance into Group Relative Policy Optimization (GRPO). By incorporating retrieval into the reinforcement learning loop, ESCRAG-R1 transforms external knowledge into a robust learning signal that stimulates explicit internal reasoning prior to generation and fundamentally reshapes the model's internal policy. To provide the reliable supervision required for this optimization, we construct ESC-Preference, a high-quality dataset based on a Client--Counselor--Judge evaluation framework that delivers precise, empathy-aware reward signals. Extensive experiments demonstrate that ESCRAG-R1 significantly outperforms existing baselines by mitigating superficial splicing and realizing a natural integration of professional guidance and empathetic expression. Code and datasets are released at https://github.com/Matcha-Liu/ESCRAG-R1.

</details>


### [100] [GuardianBench: A Same-Scene Instruction-Contrastive Benchmark for Latent Contextual Risk in Embodied AI](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.21928)
*Zhesheng Zhang,Jiahao Lu,Wei Liu,Cong Pan,Jianhua Yang,Yixiang Chen,Hongyuan Yu,Mengqi Zhang,Kailin Lyu,Zhumin Chen,Keji He*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: GuardianBench是一个基于国际安全标准构建的指令对比基准，通过3,024个同场景安全/不安全指令配对样本，揭示了具身智能VLM在"潜在上下文风险"（即场景固定、仅改变指令导致的安全性差异）上的严重缺陷：主模型平均配对准确率仅24.1%，且系统性的理由审计显示模型无法绑定区分安全与不安全组合的关键指令线索。论文提出的轻量级判罚级训练目标VLOS可显著提升开源权重模型的性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 以往工作在具身安全领域主要改变视觉场景或评估执行时动态，忽略了"固定场景、仅变化指令"这一互补维度。这类风险是潜在的：单看指令或场景都安全，但两者组合后才产生危险。需要一种受控基准来隔离和评估这一被忽视的风险维度。

Method: 1) 构建GuardianBench：基于国际安全标准，创建3,024个指令-场景样本，组织为同场景安全/不安全对比对，覆盖多种危害类别。2) 系统评估SOTA VLM，进行配对级准确率与指令敏感度分析。3) 实施系统性理由审计，定位模型失败的根因（无法绑定指令相关线索）。4) 提出VLOS（Verdict Log-Odds Supervision），一种轻量级判罚级目标，用于事后训练开源权重骨干模型。

Result: 主模型在GuardianBench上平均配对准确率仅24.1%，表现出指令不敏感的判罚行为（倾向同时批准同一场景下的两条指令）。理由审计定位核心失败模式为模型无法绑定区分安全/不安全组合的指令相关线索。VLOS显著提升了开源权重骨干模型的性能。

Conclusion: GuardianBench作为受控评估套件，明确了潜在上下文风险的任务形式化、基于标准驱动的对比基准构建、配对级与理由级失败诊断，以及基准驱动的判罚校准，为暴露和改进指令-场景组合下的安全推理提供了系统化工具。

Abstract: In embodied AI, safety risk can be latent: a benign instruction and a safe scene become hazardous only when composed. Prior work has advanced embodied safety by varying visual contexts or evaluating execution-time dynamics, but the complementary axis of fixing the scene and varying only the instruction remains underexplored. We introduce GuardianBench, an instruction-contrastive benchmark grounded in international safety standards that isolates this latent contextual risk through 3,024 instruction-scene examples organized as same-scene Safe/Unsafe contrastive pairs across various hazard categories. Benchmarking state-of-the-art vision-language models (VLMs) reveals instruction-insensitive verdicts: models disproportionately approve both instructions under a given scene; across the primary models, average pair accuracy is only 24.1%. Our systematic rationale audit localizes the dominant failure: models fail to bind the instruction-relevant cues that differentiate safe from unsafe compositions. As a post-training case study, Verdict Log-Odds Supervision (VLOS), a lightweight verdict-level objective, substantially improves performance on open-weight backbones. Together, our latent contextual risk task formulation, standards-grounded contrastive benchmark construction, pair-level and rationale-level failure diagnosis, and benchmark-enabled verdict calibration establish GuardianBench as a controlled evaluation suite for exposing and improving safety reasoning over instruction-scene compositions under latent contextual risk.

</details>


### [101] [One-Step Evolution for Long-Time Extrapolation: An Error-Bound-Informed and Prior-Guided Neural Residual Framework for Autonomous PDEs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.22026)
*Maqun Zhang,Feng Gao,Wankun Chen,Hui Yu,Yanhai Gan,Junyu Dong*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出一种基于数值先验引导、物理约束的PDE长时间外推求解方法，无需真实轨迹监督，通过控制单步演化算子的近似误差及其传播误差来实现稳定的长时间模拟，在五类基准案例中优于十种物理信息学习方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于深度学习的PDE求解方法存在明显不足：神经算子通常依赖大量轨迹数据，而物理信息方法在长时间外推中稳定性有限。对于适定的自治PDE，长时间轨迹可由固定步长演化算子的重复组合生成，因此长时间外推的精度取决于该算子近似误差及其在递归组合下的传播误差的控制。作者旨在解决无真实轨迹监督下长时间模拟精度不足的问题。

Method: 提出一种数值先验引导、物理约束的训练方法：(1) 利用低成本的数值先验降低单步演化算子逼近的难度；(2) 使用弱形式PDE残差作为单步误差项在误差传播界中的可计算代理。该方法无需真实轨迹监督，在统一训练和模型选择协议下与十种物理信息学习方法进行对比。

Result: 该方法在覆盖四类PDE的五个基准案例中均有效：在所有案例中降低了相对数值先验的长时间外推误差，并在每个案例中优于最佳对比基线方法，从而在无真实轨迹监督条件下提升了不同PDE的长时间模拟精度。

Conclusion: 数值先验引导与弱形式物理约束的相结合，可以在不依赖真实轨迹数据的情况下有效控制误差传播，显著提升长时间外推的精度和稳定性，为物理信息深度学习求解PDE提供了一种可行且高效的新范式。

Abstract: Accurate simulation of the long-time evolution of systems governed by partial differential equations (PDEs) is central to scientific computing. Among existing deep learning?based approaches for solving PDEs, neural operators typically rely on extensive trajectory data, whereas physics-informed meth?ods often exhibit limited stability during long-time extrapolation. For a well-posed autonomous PDE, long-time trajectories can be generated by repeated composition of a fixed-step evolution operator; hence, long-time extrapolation depends on controlling the approximation error of this operator and the propagation of that error under recursive composition. Accordingly, we propose a numerical-prior-guided, physics-constrained method trained without ground-truth trajectory supervision: a low-cost numerical prior reduces the difficulty of approximating the one?step evolution operator, while a weak-form PDE residual provides a computable proxy for the one-step error term in the error?propagation bound. We validate the method on five benchmark cases spanning four PDE classes and compare it with ten physics?informed learning methods under a unified protocol that excludes ground-truth trajectories from training and model selection. The results indicate that, in all five cases, the proposed method reduces long-time extrapolation error relative to the numerical prior and outperforms the best competing baseline in each case, thereby improving long-time simulation accuracy across different PDEs without ground-truth trajectory supervision. The source code developed for this paper will be made publicly available upon acceptance of the manuscript.

</details>


### [102] [MEMORY Wins All: Indirect Bias Injection Attacks via Social Media Feeds](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.22061)
*Minjae Seo,Wonwoo Choi,Geonwoo Han,Taekyoung Kwon,Yongsu Kim,Sang Seo,Jaewon Noh,Hankyul Baek,Seongyun Seo,Myoungsung You*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出IBIA（间接偏见注入攻击），通过外部内容将对手立场植入个人AI代理的持久记忆中，无需直接访问代理、记忆或用户查询即可操纵后续行为，并提出了相应的防御方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 个人AI代理在日常任务中经常摄取外部内容（如网页浏览、邮件处理、SNS摘要）并将信息存入持久记忆。作者发现这种常规的外部内容摄取行为开辟了一条间接操纵代理后续行为的路径，且无需直接访问代理本身。

Method: IBIA结合三种机制：1) 评论伪装（comment cloaking），使植入内容与周围讨论保持一致；2) 评论水印（comment watermarking），支持内容筛选时的轻量级识别；3) 类别锚定（category anchoring），使保留的立场在后续相关请求下变得突出。并在BiasBench基准上评估。

Result: 在BiasBench基准（6,000条对手构建的社交评论和120个邮件实例）上，基于水印的筛选识别出95.9%的注入评论。在OpenClaw设置下，IBIA在四个下游任务中平均达到91.2%的对手对齐响应率（AAR），其中在GPT-5.5上达到86.6%。提出的记忆边界防御可将AAR降至80.6%。

Conclusion: 个人AI代理在摄取外部内容时的记忆持久化机制存在安全漏洞，易受间接偏见注入攻击。当前防御（记忆边界防御）虽能降低攻击效果但仍不够充分，需要进一步研究更稳健的记忆安全防护机制。

Abstract: Personal AI agents routinely consume external content while performing tasks such as web browsing, email processing, and SNS feed summarization, and they retain selected information or execution results in persistent memory for later use. We show that this ordinary ingestion of external content opens an indirect path for manipulating subsequent agent behavior. Based on this observation, we present IBIA, an Indirect Bias Injection Attack that plants an adversary-aligned stance on a specific topic into a victim agent's memory through external content, without direct access to the agent, its memory, or future user queries. For this, IBIA combines three mechanisms: comment cloaking, which keeps the crafted content consistent with the surrounding discussion, comment watermarking, which enables lightweight identification during curation, and category anchoring, which makes the retained stance salient under later related requests. We evaluate IBIA on BiasBench, a benchmark of 6,000 adversary-crafted social comments and 120 email instances. The watermark-based curation identifies 95.9% of the injected comments. Under the OpenClaw setting, IBIA achieves adversary-aligned response rates (AARs) of 91.2% on average across four downstream tasks, including 86.6% on the frontier GPT-5.5. We further propose a memory boundary defense that detects the injected bias and reduces AARs to 80.6%.

</details>


### [103] [Search Broadly, Seek Evidence on Both Sides, Decide Narrowly: Evidence-Admissible GraphRAG for Longitudinal Clinical Event Verification](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.22062)
*Xingtao Lin,Yubo Feng,Weixin Liu,Hangqi Ren,Junchao Zhou,Caiwan Sun,You Chen*
