Abstract: Consider a typical test of a robot system: one observes a sequence of outcomes concerning some aspect of interest (crash or no crash, tracking error, time to completion), and reports a mean (crash risk, average error, mean time to completion) and, more importantly, an interval guaranteed to contain that mean at a prescribed confidence, referred to as a performance certificate. Given expensive real-world trials, the sample size is therefore small, and the certificate is often loose. Now consider the same procedure, except that before each real outcome is revealed, the operator ``peeks'' at a large bank of simulated results, and places a bet on where the real outcome will land. As the real outcomes settle the bets, the operator gains or loses wealth. One's ``trust'' over simulators also shifts within the portfolio. This paper develops that idea into a sim-to-real betting certificate framework with three contributions: (i) An algorithm that links a scalable bank of simulators to effective bets, and the accumulated betting wealth to the certificate. (ii) A proof that the returned certificate is anytime valid, covering the true mean with the prescribed probability, using any simulator bank. (iii) The guaranteed wealth-regret bounds yield configuration principles for the proposed algorithm and simulator bank design to deliver tight certificates. Experiments across synthetic distributions and real-world robot tests, covering both replayed standardized testing outcomes and online runtime evaluation, show the proposed method narrows the certificate by $51.6\%\pm16\%$ against classic and state-of-the-art baselines, and by $32.26\%\pm8\%$ in the extremely limited-sample regime ($\leq30$ samples).

</details>


### [138] [Force/Torque-Based Kinematic Adaptation for Robotic Manipulation Tasks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.21592)
*Carl Glen Henshaw*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出一种自适应运动学估计方案，仅利用关节角度传感和腕部力/力矩传感器，在线估计机器人与任务特征之间的未知运动学关系（如工具几何），无需外部感知工具尖端。该方案具有可证明的稳定性，并展示了刚性与柔性控制器两种情形下的收敛特性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 接触丰富的机器人操作需要精确的关节-任务特征运动学模型，但该关系常因工具更换、接触模式变化（尤其是多指手全手抓取时接触点非精确指定）而未知或瞬变。现有方法依赖外部感知或精确模型，难以适应这种不确定性。

Method: 1) 推导可证明稳定的运动学更新律，仅从力/力矩反馈辨识未知工具的运动学参数，无需外部工具尖端测量；2) 分别证明刚性与带柔顺控制器（内环）两种情况下的稳定性；3) 分析可辨识性——辨识局限于运动激励的方向；4) 将控制与估计联合问题建模为二次规划（QP），揭示其能精确产生更新律的预测项但无法复现跟踪自适应项；5) 在仿真中验证于peg-in-hole插入任务。

Result: 仿真验证了该方案在peg-in-hole插入任务中的有效性。关键发现：刚性插入推动下工具长度不可观测；柔顺环的被动让渡能部分激励可辨识性；二阶导纳条件下柔顺证明在连续时间中无条件成立；QP公式能精确给出更新的预测项但缺少跟踪自适应项。

Conclusion: 该工作是"将操作学习分解为可独立学习的任务策略（如强化学习）与在线自适应的运动学组件"这一研究计划的初步步骤，旨在使任务策略与具体机器人、手或工具解耦，实现跨平台迁移与在线适应。

Abstract: Contact-rich robotic manipulation requires an accurate model of the kinematic relationship between a robot's joints and the task features it senses. This relationship is rarely known exactly: it changes with each tool the robot picks up and shifts, sometimes almost instantaneously, as contact modes change --- especially for multi-fingered hands that make and break contact at points that are not exactly prescribed, as in full-hand grasping. This paper develops an adaptive scheme that estimates that relationship online, using only joint-angle sensing and a wrist-mounted force/torque sensor, with no exteroceptive measurement of the tool tip. We derive a provably stable kinematic update law that identifies the kinematics of an unknown tool from force/torque feedback alone, and prove stability of both the rigid case and the case with a compliance controller as an inner loop. We show that identification is confined to the directions the motion excites --- so that, for example, a tool's length is unobservable under a rigid insertion push, while a compliant loop's passive yielding partially excites it; and that with a second-order admittance the compliant certificate holds unconditionally in continuous time. We also pose the combined control and estimation problem as a Quadratic Program (QP): the formulation yields the prediction term of the update law exactly but, instructively, cannot reproduce the tracking adaptation term. We validate the scheme in simulation on a peg-in-hole insertion. This work is the first step in a research program aimed at factoring manipulation learning into a task policy which can be learned in isolation of the robot, for instance by reinforcement learning, and an adaptive kinematic component that adapts online to the particular robot, hand, or tool in use.

</details>


### [139] [Why Personalization Matters: Cross-Subject Challenges in EMG-IMU-based HRI Activity Recognition](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.21620)
*Ruan Rithelle Chagas de Faria Carminati,Giovanni Braglia,Luigi Biagiotti,Ronnier Frates Rohrich,Andre Schneider de Oliveira,Mikael Nedel Hartmann,André Eugenio Lazzaretti*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出了一个名为MAGIC-HRI的多模态数据集，基于Myo臂环采集EMG和IMU信号，覆盖53类运动（含LIBRAS手语数字、手势、物品交接、工具操作及装配/空闲动作），由11名参与者采集。研究采用EMG能量包络进行活动分割，结合滑动窗口与频域/时域特征，通过交叉验证网格搜索调优多个经典分类器，其中随机森林表现最佳。LOSO评估揭示了显著的泛化差距（主体依赖性），而个性化微调实验表明注入少量新用户样本可显著提升识别性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 为了支持人机交互（HRI）中的物体交接与装配场景，需要可穿戴设备实现人类活动与手势的鲁棒识别。现有数据集在面向HRI的运动类别覆盖上存在不足，且对模型跨受试者泛化能力的系统评估较为缺乏。

Method: 使用Myo臂环采集11名参与者的EMG与IMU信号，构建包含53种运动类别的MAGIC-HRI数据集；通过EMG能量包络检测肌肉激活进行信号分割，采用滑动窗口处理并提取时频域特征；使用交叉验证网格搜索调优多个经典分类器（随机森林为最强基线），采用LOSO协议评估跨受试者泛化，并进行个性化适应实验验证小样本注入的增益。

Result: LOSO评估显示出较大的泛化差距，表明模型存在显著的主体依赖性；个性化适应实验结果表明，向新用户模型中注入少量样本可显著改善识别准确率，验证了个性化的必要性。

Conclusion: 该研究贡献了一个面向HRI的广泛多模态数据集、强调泛化性能的严格评估框架，并提供了实证证据表明个性化对于实际HRI场景中的可靠部署是必要的。

Abstract: This paper investigates wearable-based recognition of human activities and gestures to support Human-Robot Interaction (HRI) in object-handover and assembly-like scenarios. Electromyography (EMG) and Inertial Measurement Unit (IMU) signals were collected using a Myo armband, culminating in a novel dataset introduced as MAGIC-HRI (Multimodal Activity, Gesture and Intention Collection) with a large taxonomy of 53 movement classes, including Brazilian Sign Language (LIBRAS) numbers (0-9), hand gestures, object/tool handover actions (pick up/give/hold), tool-manipulation tasks, and generic assembly/idle motions, collected from 11 participants with 10 samples per class (530 samples per participant). Signals are segmented by detecting muscle activation via an EMG energy envelope, then processed using sliding windows; time- and frequency-domain features are extracted. Multiple classical classifiers are tuned via cross-validated grid search, with Random Forest as the strongest baseline. A Leave-One-Subject-Out (LOSO) protocol reveals a large generalization gap, indicating substantial subject dependence. A personalized adaptation experiment suggests that injecting a small number of samples from a new user can markedly improve recognition. Overall, the study contributes a broad, HRI-driven multimodal dataset, a rigorous evaluation emphasizing generalization, and practical evidence that personalization is likely required for robust deployment in practical HRI.

</details>


### [140] [Lifelong Robot Recomposition via Persistent Categorical Modeling for Unified Task-Driven Co-Design, Verification, and Planning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.21676)
*Steven Swanbeck,Mitch Pryor*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出一种基于严格对称幺半范畴的组合式框架，将机器人系统形式化为抽象电路，通过SMT求解器在设计和运行时同时综合硬件、软件与行为的组合，并支持整个生命周期内的查询（如Pareto前沿、不可行诊断、最小恢复与有限变更重组），在搜索救援场景中展示了端到端能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统机器人系统在静态配置下设计部署，设计时的假设在运行时成为不可变约束，导致系统在运行条件变化（自身、任务或环境变化）时变得脆弱，缺乏适应性和生命周期级别的可维护性。

Method: 在严格对称幺半范畴中将机器人系统形式化为抽象电路；利用幺半函子将系统投影到不同生命周期视图；通过基于SMT的求解器同时综合设计时的硬件/软件/行为组合与运行时的组件规格和参数求解；模型支持Pareto前沿映射、不可行性诊断、最小修复以及有限变更重组等查询。

Result: 在真实部署机器人上，与官方实现的优化数值规划器、流式规划器和SMT规划器进行对比评估，并在搜索救援场景中端到端展示了机器人识别自身不适配、综合并采用新的整体配置以恢复运行的完整流程。

Conclusion: 该组合式持久模型超越了传统"计划存在性"范畴，提升了机器人在生命周期中的适应性与自我修复能力，且求解器和软件已开源，为该方向提供了可复现的基础。

Abstract: Robotic systems are traditionally designed and deployed in static configurations, with assumptions made at design-time becoming immutable constraints during runtime. This design-then-deploy paradigm produces performant systems under narrow operating conditions, but renders robots brittle when qualities of themselves, their tasks, or their environments unexpectedly change. We address this challenge with a compositional framework that formalizes robotic systems as abstract circuits within a strict symmetric monoidal category, in which design and runtime composition of hardware, software, and behavior are synthesized simultaneously via an SMT-based solver, with monoidal functors projecting the system into lifecycle-specific views and free symbolic variables simultaneously solving for parameters and entire component specifications within larger compositions. This persistent model also supports queries a long-lived system needs beyond plan existence across its entire lifecycle, including mapping Pareto fronts over candidate compositions, diagnosing why a composition has become infeasible, finding its minimal restoration, and reconfiguring with limited change to the deployed system. We evaluate against official implementations of optimal numeric, stream-based, and SMT-based planners all measured onboard a deployed robot and demonstrate the approach end-to-end in a search-and-rescue scenario in which the robot recognizes when it has become unfit and synthesizes and assumes new holistic configurations to restore operation. We release our solver and supporting software open-source.

</details>


### [141] [CounterAlign: Counterfactual Supervision for Vision-Language-Action Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.21740)
*Haru Kondoh,Kei Ota,Asako Kanezaki,Yueh-Hua Wu*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出一种无需额外标注或非专家数据即可为VLA模型提供密集纠正信号的方法：通过指令重标注将专家示范转换为反事实的指令-观测-动作元组，并结合对抗判别器训练学习指令约束的奖励模型，用于离线强化学习，在LIBERO-PRO基准和真实机器人上均优于强基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: VLA模型通常采用行为克隆训练，但行为克隆只提供专家动作的正向监督，缺乏明确指示哪些动作与指令不一致或不合适的负向监督。强化学习虽可提供纠正信号，但依赖外部定义的奖励或精心整理的非专家数据，这两者在机器人领域获取成本高昂。作者希望在不依赖额外数据的条件下，从现有专家示范中挖掘密集的纠正性监督。

Method: 方法核心包含两部分：(1) 指令重标注——将专家示范中的动作与不匹配的替代指令配对，从数据集中合成反事实的指令-观测-动作元组；(2) 对抗判别器训练——利用合成的反事实数据训练与指令相关的奖励模型，用于离线强化学习。整个过程无需额外rollout或人工标注。

Result: 在面向鲁棒性的LIBERO-PRO基准上，该方法在目标位置和任务扰动方面优于强SOTA基线；在真实机器人实验（TX-G2，兼容AGIBot G2）上也胜过竞争性基线。

Conclusion: 在数据受限的VLA学习场景中，从每条示范中提取更密集的监督信号可以作为收集更多数据的补充方案，有效提升模型鲁棒性。

Abstract: Vision-Language-Action (VLA) models are typically trained with behavior cloning (BC) on expert demonstrations. However, BC provides only positive supervision for expert actions, without explicit negative supervision indicating which actions are instruction-inconsistent or otherwise inappropriate. Reinforcement learning (RL) can provide such corrective signals, but often relies on externally specified rewards or curated non-expert data, both of which are costly to obtain in robotics. We show that offline RL for VLA models need not rely on curated non-expert trajectories: successful expert demonstrations alone can be transformed into dense corrective supervision through instruction relabeling. Specifically, by pairing expert actions with mismatched alternative instructions, we synthesize counterfactual instruction-observation-action tuples from the dataset and combine them with adversarial discriminator training to learn an instruction-grounded reward model for offline RL, without collecting additional rollouts or annotations. On the robustness-focused LIBERO-PRO benchmark, our method improves robustness to object position and task perturbations over a strong state-of-the-art baseline. It also outperforms competitive baselines in real-robot experiments on the TX-G2 (compatible with AGIBot G2). More broadly, our results suggest that, for data-constrained VLA learning, extracting denser supervision from each demonstration can complement collecting additional data.

</details>


### [142] [Stakeholder Insights for Designing In-Home Social Robots for Dementia Disorientation Detection and Caregiver-Aware Intervention](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.22008)
*Emmanuel Akinrintoyo,Nicole Salomons*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文通过对14位痴呆症照护者与从业者的半结构化访谈，探讨了居家痴呆症患者（PwDs）的定向障碍现象，并提出了社交辅助机器人在日常情境中检测与管理定向障碍的设计启示。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 定向障碍是痴呆症患者常见且令人痛苦的体验，会增加焦虑、躁动和安全风险。虽然辅助技术已探索提醒、监测和活动支持，但社交辅助机器人如何支持日常情境中定向障碍的检测与管理仍鲜有研究，这一领域作为社会技术挑战尚未得到充分探讨。

Method: 采用质性研究方法，对14位痴呆症照护者和从业者进行半结构化访谈，样本涵盖家庭照护者、专业照护者、职业治疗师、心理健康从业者、痴呆症专科护士以及健康与技术负责人。

Result: 研究发现定向障碍具有反复性和波动性特征，常通过重复提问、活动时间不当和日常规律紊乱等行为线索显现。照护者认为定向问题带有情绪敏感性，直接纠正可能加剧痛苦。参与者普遍对机器人辅助持接受态度，但前提是其定位是"支持性"而非"纠正性"。

Conclusion: 基于研究发现，本文提出了社交辅助机器人的关键设计启示：提供情境感知的定向支持、融入日常规律，并通过及时升级机制支持照护者。

Abstract: Disorientation is a common and distressing experience for people living with dementia. It often manifests as confusion about time, place, or personal context. These episodes can increase anxiety, agitation, and safety risks, especially for persons with dementia (PwDs) who live independently at home. While assistive technologies have explored reminders, monitoring, and activity support, little research has explored how socially assistive robots can support the detection and management of disorientation in everyday living contexts. Hence, disorientation detection and intervention remain under-examined as socio-technical challenges. We conducted 14 semi-structured interviews with dementia caregivers and practitioners, including family and professional caregivers, occupational therapists, mental health practitioners, dementia nurse practitioners, and well-being and technology leads. The findings reveal that recurrent and fluctuating. It often emerges through behavioural cues such as repeated questioning, inappropriate activity timing and disrupted daily routines. Caregivers described orientation as emotionally charged, and direct correction may increase distress. The participants were generally receptive to robotic assistance when framed as supportive rather than corrective. Based on the insights, we identify essential design implications for socially assistive robots that provide context-aware orientation support, integrate into daily routines and support caregivers through timely escalation.

</details>


### [143] [MotionDLO: Hybrid Event- and Frame-Based Tracking of Deformable Linear Objects](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.22398)
*Annalena Hartmann,Priyamvada Ajithkumar,Patrick Bründl,Jörg Franke*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: MotionDLO是一个基于事件相机与帧相机混合架构的实时可变形线性物体（DLO）跟踪框架，通过结合分割技术与相干点漂移（CPD）算法，实现了12ms更新率、0.43mm点-曲线误差的高精度、时间一致的DLO形状跟踪，支持机器人操作中的动态路径自适应。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 可靠跟踪动态可变形线性物体（DLO）时，同时保证鲁棒性、准确性和时间一致的状态估计是机器人感知中的基础挑战。现有基于事件的方法虽计算高效但精度低于基于帧的方法，而基于帧的方法又牺牲了事件稀疏性。需要一个混合架构来解决这一精度与效率之间的权衡。

Method: 提出了混合事件-帧双模态跟踪架构：事件流利用事件相机的高时间分辨率和稀疏性提供高频运动更新，帧信息用于稳定空间精度和物体身份识别。方法结合分割技术与相干点漂移（CPD）算法，基于运动相干理论实现时间一致的形状估计，同时保持低计算开销。

Result: 实验验证了框架的实时性能，达到12ms更新率，形状跟踪精度为0.43mm点-曲线误差，能可靠地在视频序列中关联DLO实例，支持操作过程中的动态路径适应。

Conclusion: MotionDLO成功融合事件与帧两种传感模态的互补优势，在保持实时性和计算效率的同时实现了高精度、时间一致的DLO跟踪，为机器人精细操作任务提供了可靠的感知基础，且代码与数据已公开。

Abstract: Reliably tracking moving deformable linear objects (DLOs) while simultaneously ensuring robustness, accuracy, and temporally consistent state estimation remains a fundamental challenge in robot perception. We introduce MotionDLO, a real-time tracking framework specifically designed to overcome these limitations in temporal continuity and latency. The method exploits the high temporal resolution and sparsity of event-based cameras and combines segmentation with the Coherent Point Drift (CPD) algorithm under the principles of Motion Coherence Theory. This integration enables temporally consistent shape estimation while maintaining a low computational overhead. Existing event-based tracking methods are typically computationally efficient but exhibit reduced accuracy compared to frame-based approaches, or alternatively compromise event sparsity to achieve competitive performance. To resolve this trade-off, we propose a hybrid event- and frame-based tracking architecture that preserves the complementary strengths of both sensing modalities. The event stream ensures high-frequency motion updates, while frame-based information stabilizes spatial accuracy and object identity. We demonstrate that the proposed framework reliably associates DLO instances across video sequences, enabling robust perception for robotic manipulation tasks. Experimental results validate real-time performance at 12 ms update rates and accurate shape tracking with an point-to-curve error as measurement of accuracy of up to 0.43 mm, supporting dynamic path adaptation during manipulation. The source code and demonstration datasets are publicly available.

</details>


### [144] [LD4WAM: Learning Latent Dynamics from Human Videos for World Action Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.22403)
*Zhenhao Shen,Jiaqi Liang,Jasper Lu,Feng Jiang,Yuran Wang,Chuanbo Wei,Jiayi Liu,Jianchun Yang,Qize Yu,Jiadi You,Ce Hao,Guanqi He,Chen Xie,Ruihai Wu*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出了LD4WAM框架，通过运动对齐的潜空间动力学作为体素无关的表示，将人类视频先验与低级机器人动作桥接，在RoboTwin仿真和真实机器人上表现出色。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 人类视频因多样性和低成本日益成为训练世界动作模型的核心数据，但现有方法存在两难：预测像素级未来帧得到不可直接执行的动力学，而运动重定向得到可直接执行的动作却存在跨体现的视觉差距。

Method: 提出运动对齐的潜空间动力学（motion-aligned latent dynamics）作为体现无关表示；构建LD4WAM框架，包含一个用语义重建和真实运动对齐训练的潜动力学模型，以及一个混合变换器（MoT）架构的世界动力学动作模型，通过可学习查询从生成的未来帧中蒸馏潜动力学用于动作条件化。在超过5000小时的人类和机器人数据上预训练。

Result: LD4WAM在RoboTwin仿真和配备夹爪及灵巧手的真实机器人上均表现强劲，并能很好地泛化到未见过的物体和背景。

Conclusion: 运动对齐的潜空间动力学有效弥合了视频先验与低级动作之间的差距，验证了该框架在跨体现动作生成上的可行性。

Abstract: Human video is playing an increasingly central role in training World Action Models (WAMs), owing to its diversity and low collection cost relative to teleoperated robot data. However, most WAMs learn from such video only by predicting pixel-level future frames, giving dynamics that are not directly actionable, whereas motion retargeting recovers directly actionable actions but leaves a large visual gap across embodiments. We therefore propose motion-aligned latent dynamics as an embodiment-agnostic representation to bridge video priors and low-level actions. We further present LD4WAM, which pairs a Latent Dynamics Model trained with semantic reconstruction and real motion alignment with a World Dynamics Action Model built as a mixture-of-transformers (MoT), which preserves full future-video generation and uses learnable queries to distill these latent dynamics from generated futures for action conditioning. Pretrained on our curated unified dataset of over 5{,}000 hours of human and robot data, LD4WAM performs strongly in RoboTwin simulation and on real robots equipped with both grippers and dexterous hands, while generalizing well to unseen objects and backgrounds.

</details>


### [145] [Robust Bimanual Vision-Language-Action Models via Embarrassingly Simple Modality Masking](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.22419)
*Dongzhou Cheng,Ziang Li,Yixiao Zhou,Haojuan Li,Jinghao Zhang,Lei Lei,Minjing Dong,Jie Gui,Jiaqi Wang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出一种名为"模态掩码机制"(M3)的仅训练期策略，通过在训练过程中随机遮蔽多模态通道子集，提升基于查询的视觉-语言-动作(VLA)模型在双臂机器人操作中的鲁棒性，在RoboTwin 2.0和真实世界任务中均取得了显著的性能提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 基于查询的VLA模型虽然具有低延迟推理优势，适合双臂操作，但在复杂双臂任务中仍会出现不连续动作和执行失败。作者假设不稳定的多视角与语言融合是失败的原因之一，注意力往往会扩散到干扰区域。

Method: 提出Modality Masking Mechanism (M3)，一种极其简单、仅训练期使用的策略。在训练过程中随机遮蔽部分模态通道，让策略暴露于受控的部分观测下，从而减少对干扰线索的依赖，更多地依赖可靠证据。该方法不需要修改网络架构，也不需要大规模机器人预训练。

Result: 在RoboTwin 2.0的十个双臂任务中，与Adapter基线相比，M3在Clean设置下平均成功率提升21.7%，在Clean2Rand设置下提升11.4%；在三个长时程真实世界任务中，全任务平均成功率提升超过30%。

Conclusion: 结构化训练期掩码是提升基于查询的VLA策略在双臂操作中鲁棒性的一种实用且有效的方法，能够在不增加推理开销和架构改动的情况下显著改善任务成功率。

Abstract: Query-based Vision-Language-Action (VLA) models offer low-latency inference that is attractive for bimanual robotic manipulation, but we observe that they can still exhibit discontinuous actions and execution failures in complex dual-arm tasks. We hypothesize that unstable multi-view and language fusion is one contributing factor in these failures, often coinciding with attention spreading to distracting regions. To improve robustness, we introduce the Modality Masking Mechanism (M3), an embarrassingly simple, training-only strategy that requires no architectural changes or large-scale robot pretraining. M3 stochastically masks subsets of modality channels during training, exposing the policy to controlled partial observations and encouraging it to rely less on distracting cues and more on evidence that remains reliable. We evaluate M3 on ten bimanual tasks from RoboTwin 2.0 and on three long-horizon real-world tasks. Compared with the Adapter baseline, M3 improves average success by 21.7% in the Clean setting and 11.4% in Clean2Rand, where policies are trained on clean demonstrations and evaluated on randomized scenes, while also improving averaged real-world full-task success by over 30%. These results suggest that structured training-time masking is a practical way to improve the robustness of query-based VLA policies for bimanual manipulation.

</details>


### [146] [EMPIRE: Explicit Manipulation Planning as a Learnable Intermediate Representation for Egocentric Hand-Motion Forecasting](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.22449)
*Wen Wang,Ruibing Hou,Hong Chang,Shiguang Shan,Xilin Chen*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出EMPIRE，一个两阶段框架，将显式操作规划作为中间表征用于第一人称视角的灵巧手部运动预测。框架分为"学习规划"和"学习动作"两个阶段：先学习显式操作规划以捕捉手物交互过程，再在冻结的规划器表征条件下合成未来双手手部运动，避免运动生成梯度干扰操作规划。同时构建了包含650,910个训练窗口、覆盖111个任务的EMPIRE-651K大规模双手手部运动数据集，在相同训练和评估协议下达到SOTA精度（MPJPE 84.53mm，手指相对误差38.97mm）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的基于VLM的方法通常直接将观测映射到未来运动，忽视了控制手物交互的底层操作过程；同时端到端优化将操作学习与运动合成耦合，导致运动生成梯度干扰预训练的操作感知表征。这些局限影响了手部运动预测的准确性和可解释性。

Method: 提出EMPIRE两阶段框架。第一阶段"学习规划"（Learn to Plan）：从多模态上下文中学习显式操作规划，捕捉手物交互的进展过程。第二阶段"学习动作"（Learn to Act）：运动生成器在冻结的规划器表征条件下合成未来的双手手部运动，防止运动生成梯度影响操作规划。同时构建EMPIRE-651K数据集，包含650,910个训练窗口、111个任务，每个任务配有逐手显式操作规划。

Result: 在相同训练和评估协议下，EMPIRE达到最先进的手部运动预测精度，MPJPE为84.53mm，手指相对误差为38.97mm。

Conclusion: 显式操作规划作为中间表征能够有效解耦操作学习与运动合成，提升第一人称灵巧手部运动预测的准确性和泛化能力；代码和数据集已公开于GitHub。

Abstract: Forecasting dexterous hand motions from egocentric observations is fundamental to intelligent interactive systems. Existing VLM-based methods typically map observations directly to future motions, overlooking the underlying manipulation process that governs hand-object interactions. Moreover, end-to-end optimization couples manipulation learning with motion synthesis, causing motion-generation gradients to interfere with the pre-learned manipulation-aware representations. To overcome these limitations, we propose EMPIRE, a two-stage framework that introduces Explicit Manipulation Planning as an Intermediate Representation for Egocentric hand-motion forecasting. Stage I: Learn to Plan. EMPIRE first learns explicit manipulation plans from multimodal context to capture the progression of hand-object interactions. Stage II: Learn to Act. A motion generator synthesizes future bimanual hand motions conditioned on frozen planner representations, preventing motion-generation gradients from affecting manipulation planning. To support our method, we further construct EMPIRE-651K, a bimanual hand-motion forecasting dataset comprising 650,910 training windows across 111 tasks, each paired with an explicit per-hand manipulation plan. Under identical training and evaluation protocols, EMPIRE achieves state-of-the-art forecasting accuracy, with an MPJPE of 84.53 mm and a finger-relative error of 38.97mm. We release the code and dataset at https://github.com/wangwen-banban/EMPIRE.

</details>


### [147] [A Unified Neural-Aided Alignment and Calibration Method for AUVs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.22496)
*Guy Damari,Zeev Yampolsky,Itzik Klein*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出一种全神经辅助的自主水下机器人（AUV）多普勒测速仪（DVL）初始化流水线，用ResAlignNet和DCNet两个互补神经网络分别替代传统对准（alignment）和校准（calibration）两阶段，可在单一近匀速轨迹上原位运行，仅需25秒数据即可完成初始化，在5种真实传感器误差组合下将速度均方根误差较模型基线平均降低68.7%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统DVL初始化依赖模型算法，需要复杂机动、水面卫星参考测量和简化误差模型，导致初始化耗时长、依赖轨迹且对传感器质量敏感。作者旨在摆脱这些限制，提出一种不依赖复杂机动和外部参考的神经网络方案。

Method: 提出统一的神经网络DVL初始化流水线：采用ResAlignNet神经网络完成INS与DVL坐标系间的旋转对准，采用DCNet神经网络估计DVL误差项，取代传统的基于模型的两阶段算法。该流水线在单一近匀速轨迹上原位运行，且仅使用与模型基线相同的输入信号。

Result: 在5种不同传感器误差项组合的真实数据上，所提流水线仅用25秒初始化数据，将速度均方根误差较模型基线平均降低68.7%。

Conclusion: 全神经辅助的DVL初始化流水线能显著优于传统模型基线，大幅简化初始化流程，缩短初始化时间（仅需25秒），并降低对车辆机动模式与外部参考测量的依赖，为AUV导航初始化提供了更高效鲁棒的方案。

Abstract: Autonomous underwater vehicles (AUVs) rely on the fusion of inertial navigation systems (INS) and Doppler velocity logs (DVL) for accurate navigation. Before deployment, this fusion requires a DVL initialization pipeline consisting of two stages: alignment, which estimates the rotation between the INS and DVL frames, and calibration, which estimates the DVL error terms. Conventionally, both stages are solved with model-based algorithms that demand complex vehicle maneuvers, surface-level satellite reference measurements, and simplified error models, making initialization time-consuming, trajectory-dependent, and sensitive to sensor quality. In this work, we propose a fully neural- aided DVL initialization pipeline that replaces both stages with two complementary neural networks: ResAlignNet for alignment and DCNet for calibration. The unified pipeline operates in situ on a single nearly constant-velocity trajectory and uses the same inputs as the model-based baseline. Using real-world data recorded across five distinct sensor error-term combinations, the proposed pipeline reduces the velocity root mean squared error by an average of 68.7% over the model-based baseline, using only 25s of data for initialization.

</details>


### [148] [WorldToken: Time-First Sequence Modeling for Robotic Imitation Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.22591)
*Chunkai Yang,Andong Yang,Chao Gao*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出WorldToken，一种时间优先（time-first）的机器人策略实例化方法，将每个决策步内的多视角图像、本体感知和任务条件融合为一个"世界令牌"（world token），由因果时序Transformer建模，并用扩散动作头生成动作块。在23个RoboCasa任务上，8530万参数的策略（除冻结的CLIP文本编码器外从头训练）在每任务2900条生成演示下达到59.45%的平均闭环成功率。实验还系统刻画了数据规模、模型规模、历史截断的影响。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 机器人策略在每个决策步接收异构观测（多视角图像、本体感知、任务条件），但现有序列模型对输入的时间组织方式各不相同，缺乏对"时间优先"序列组织的系统验证。

Method: WorldToken将每个策略时间步内的异构观测融合为单一世界令牌，送入因果时序Transformer编码，并通过扩散动作头输出动作块。进行完整因子扫描（5种数据集规模×5种模型规模×2种训练种子），并测试历史截断对闭环成功率的影响。

Result: 在23个RoboCasa任务上达到59.45%平均闭环成功率；额外目标域数据带来一致增益，超过中等模型规模后收益递减；将所有50个策略的可见历史截断至1-2个时间步均降低闭环成功率；在RMBench Blocks Ranking上，将可见历史从146秒缩减至8秒使评估者成功率从95%降至28%，而探索性扩展rollout可持续参考交换序列超过850秒。

Conclusion: 研究证实了完整WorldToken实例化的经验可行性，并刻画了其在数据扩展和时序上下文方面的行为特征，但未证明其优于其他序列组织方式，也未隔离出是哪一组件导致了观测到的性能。

Abstract: Robot policies receive heterogeneous observations at each decision step, yet sequence models differ in how they organize these inputs over time. We introduce WorldToken, a time-first policy instantiation that fuses multiview images, proprioception, and task conditioning within each policy timestep into one world token. A causal temporal Transformer models the resulting world-token sequence, and a diffusion action head generates action chunks. On 23 RoboCasa tasks, an 85.3M-parameter policy trained from scratch apart from a frozen pretrained CLIP text encoder achieves 59.45% mean closed-loop success using 2,900 generated demonstrations per task. A complete factorial sweep over five dataset sizes, five model sizes, and two training seeds shows consistent gains from additional target-domain data and diminishing returns beyond moderate model size. Under same-checkpoint history truncation, reducing visible history to one or two policy timesteps lowers closed-loop success for all 50 RoboCasa policies. On RMBench Blocks Ranking, reducing visible history from 146 to 8 seconds lowers evaluator success from 95% to 28%, while an exploratory extended rollout sustains the reference swap sequence for over 850 seconds. These results establish the empirical feasibility of the complete WorldToken instantiation and characterize its data-scaling and temporal-context behavior under the tested recipes. They do not establish superiority over alternative sequence organizations or isolate which components of the complete implementation drive the observed performance.

</details>


### [149] [Physical Agentic AI: An Architecture for Orchestrating a Robot Crew with LLMs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.22657)
*Xinyuan Liu,Eren Sadikoglu,Riana Chatterjee,Ransalu Senanayake*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出Physical Agentic AI框架，在语义规划与物理执行之间引入显式架构接口，通过确定性的机器人编排器对每个技能调用逐条验证授权，将技能接地率从51%提升至96%，并将故障动作跨入执行边界的比例从100%（其中物理运动占75%）降至0%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有具身智能体框架虽能利用能力、物理前提和跨机器人协调信息提高语义接地，但无法消除不可行、时机错误或不安全的物理动作。物理机器人团队需要在规划与执行之间建立显式的架构接口，对每个计划动作在执行前根据机器人能力、系统状态和工作流约束进行验证。

Method: 提出Physical Agentic AI框架：每个机器人暴露类型化的可执行技能库，基础模型规划器将任务分解为阶段并将每个阶段分配给机器人-技能对。系统包含非执行性的任务规划器（访问技能库、机器人状态、命名位置和工作流契约）与确定性的机器人编排器（逐技能验证并授权），形成"检索提升接地+确定性门控保证执行安全"的双层架构。

Result: 在无人机-UGV搜索派遣任务（全部在Gazebo中实时执行）和人形-四足运输任务（硬件等效接口+Unitree G1和Go2物理试验）上进行评估。检索将技能接地从51%提升至96%，但信息充分的规划器仍派发23-29%的故障步骤；逐派遣强制将错误派发降至0%且无误阻断。消融实验证明是门控而非计划变化起作用。物理执行中，未加强制时8个注入故障全部跨越编排边界且6个产生机器人运动，加强制后全部在执行前被拒绝。

Conclusion: 仅靠增强规划器的语义知识不足以保证物理安全，必须在规划与执行之间设置确定性验证门控。Physical Agentic AI框架通过"语义接地+确定性编排授权"的双层设计，实现了对物理机器人群组的可靠编排，验证了显式架构边界对安全执行的必要性。

Abstract: Agentic AI frameworks interpret open-ended task goals and decompose them into multi-step plans. Richer information about embodiment-specific capabilities, physical preconditions, and cross-robot coordination improves grounding, but does not eliminate infeasible, mistimed, or unsafe physical actions. Physical robot crews therefore require an explicit architectural interface between semantic planning and execution, where every planned action is verified against robot capabilities, system state, and workflow constraints before actuation. This paper introduces Physical Agentic AI, a framework for skill-grounded robot agent orchestration, in which each robot exposes a typed library of executable skills while a foundation model planner decomposes a task into phases and assigns each phase to a robot-skill pair. A Robot Orchestration layer exposes the skill library, robot state, named locations, and workflow contracts to a non-actuating Mission Planner, while a deterministic Robot Orchestrator validates and authorizes one skill at a time. We evaluate on a drone-UGV search-and-dispatch mission, where every mission in every condition is executed live in Gazebo, and on a humanoid-quadruped transportation task using hardware-equivalent skill interfaces plus two physical trials on a Unitree G1 and Go2. Varying planner knowledge and runtime enforcement independently, we find that retrieval raises skill grounding from 51% to 96% yet leaves informed planners dispatching 23-29% of faulted steps. Per-dispatch enforcement reduces false dispatch to 0% with no false blocks, and a held-plan ablation confirms that the gate, not plan variation, is responsible. Live execution makes the difference physical: without enforcement all eight injected faults crossed the orchestration boundary and six produced robot motion; with enforcement all eight were refused before motion.

</details>


### [150] [VikPath: A Vision Kansformer Framework for Effective Obstacle Avoidance in Self-Supervised Pathfinding](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.22675)
*Junyao Wang,Yulin Xu,Mohammad Abdullah Al Faruque*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出Design，一个自监督路径规划框架，通过新颖的Vision Kansformer模块学习障碍物分布表示（无需标注轨迹），并引入急转弯惩罚项以同时优化障碍物间距和路径平滑度。实验表明，与SOTA方法相比，Design在障碍物间距上平均提升3.28%，推理延迟降低87.07%，同时保持路径平滑性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统基于启发式的路径规划算法（如A*）在复杂障碍环境中搜索效率下降；现有学习方法依赖监督信号（由传统规划器或人工标注生成），性能受限于监督质量，且主要优化路径长度而忽略障碍物间距和轨迹平滑度，导致生成路径在实际环境中可能难以安全执行。

Method: 提出Design自监督路径规划框架，核心是新颖的Vision Kansformer模块，无需标注轨迹即可学习障碍物分布表征；同时引入急转弯惩罚项以鼓励生成更平滑、更可执行的路径。

Result: 与SOTA方法相比，Design在障碍物间距上平均提高3.28%，推理延迟降低87.07%，同时保持平滑路径生成。

Conclusion: Design通过自监督学习和障碍物-平滑度联合优化，在复杂环境中有效提升了路径规划的实用性（更大安全裕度）和效率（更低推理延迟），验证了无需监督标签的路径表示学习的可行性。

Abstract: Pathfinding is a fundamental problem in artificial intelligence and autonomous systems. Traditional heuristic-based algorithms, such as A*, rely on predefined heuristic functions to guide the search process. Although effective in structured environments, their search efficiency can degrade substantially in complex, obstacle-rich scenarios, where handcrafted heuristics may provide limited guidance. Recent studies have explored learning-based approaches to improve pathfinding efficiency; however, most existing methods rely on supervised learning and require labels generated by conventional planners or obtained through manual annotation. As a result, their performance is inherently influenced by the quality of the underlying supervision and may degrade when the labeling heuristics fail to capture complex environmental structures. Moreover, existing methods primarily optimize for path length while paying limited attention to obstacle clearance and trajectory smoothness, which can lead to paths that are difficult or unsafe to execute in real-world environments. To address these limitations, we propose $\Design$, a self-supervised pathfinding framework that jointly considers obstacle proximity and path smoothness. At its core, our novel \textit{Vision Kansformer} module learns representations of obstacle distributions without relying on labeled trajectories, enabling the model to better adapt to complex environments. We further introduce a sharp-turn penalty to encourage smoother and more practically executable paths. Extensive experiments demonstrate that, compared with state-of-the-art (SOTA) approaches, $\Design$ achieves an average of 3.28\% greater obstacle clearance and 87.07\% lower inference latency while maintaining smooth path generation.

</details>


### [151] [Physics Filtering Favors the Generalization of Robot Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.22701)
*Jindou Jia,Shixuan Han,Meng Wang,Gen Li,Zihan Yang,Sicheng Zhou,Kexin Guo,Jianfei Yang,Xiang Yu,Wei Wang,Lei Guo*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出PhyFilter，一种模型无关的轻量级反馈机制模块，通过物理过滤的学习残差来纠正机器人学习输出，使机器人在动力学不确定性下即使使用有限训练数据也能有效泛化，替代大规模数据扩展策略。在四足机器人、无人机、空中机械臂及加速度微分器等系统中均验证了其有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 生物体通过物理结构和终身反馈驱动学习展现出对新环境的非凡适应能力，而现有机器人泛化方法依赖大规模数据扩展，但真实世界中采集海量机器人演示数据成本过高且缓慢，不可行，因此需要探索替代途径。

Method: 提出PhyFilter模块，通过物理过滤的学习残差纠正学习输出；该模块轻量级、模型无关，参数可通过自动学习算法优化，无需手动调参，可无缝集成到多种机器人策略中。

Result: 在四类机器人系统中验证：四足机器人泛化到未知地形、载荷变化和速度范围；无人机在未知风扰下飞行；空中机械臂在风和质量不确定下实现厘米级空中抓取；加速度微分器在分布偏移下保持鲁棒。

Conclusion: 物理过滤反馈机制可作为大规模数据扩展的强大替代方案，使机器人在有限数据下仍能实现跨环境泛化。

Abstract: Living organisms exhibit extraordinary adaptability to unseen environments through their intrinsic physical structures and lifelong feedback-driven learning. Endowing robots with comparable generalization is critical for reliable operation in the real world. While recent approaches attempt to improve generalization by scaling training data, such strategies remain impractical for robotics, where collecting real-world demonstrations at the scale of large language models is prohibitively costly and slow. Contrary to this reliance on massive datasets, we show that robots can generalize effectively under dynamics uncertainties even with limited training data by leveraging a feedback mechanism, namely PhyFilter, that corrects learning outputs with physics-filtered learning residuals. PhyFilter operates as a lightweight, model-agnostic module whose parameters can be automatically optimized through an auto-learning algorithm, eliminating manual tuning and enabling seamless integration with diverse robot policies. We validate PhyFilter across four representative robotic systems, demonstrating that it enables quadruped robots to generalize to unseen terrains, payload variations, and speed ranges; drones to flight under unseen wind disturbances; aerial manipulators to achieve centimeter-level in-air capture despite wind and mass uncertainties; and acceleration differentiators to remain robust with distribution shift. These results show that physics-filtered feedback can serve as a powerful alternative to massive data scaling.

</details>


### [152] [Triplet2Track: A Hierarchical System with Object-Centric Representations for Reliable Long-Horizon Manipulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.22800)
*Jianxiang Liu,Gaojing Zhang,Chuan Wen,Qipeng Liu,Yuxuan Zhao,Ning Guo,Wenzhao Lian*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出Triplet-to-Track System (TTS)，一种闭环长时程机器人操作模仿学习系统，利用人类视频减少对机器人采集数据的依赖，以实例级三元组表示高层子目标，将其转化为连续轨迹先验进行执行，并基于观测在线监控与重规划，在多种真实世界长时程任务中达到74.8%的平均成功率，支持物体级别和组合泛化。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有长时程机器人操作在不确定环境中难以保证可靠性：端到端VLA模型数据需求大且不透明，难以诊断和验证；层级式流水线虽然更具可解释性，但其规划缺乏对观测的强接地、与低层动作的对齐较弱，且缺乏在线反馈，导致开环行为和幻觉问题。

Method: TTS系统包含三个关键组件：(1) 将高层子目标表示为实例级接地三元组（instance-grounded triplets），增强规划与观测的接地；(2) 将三元组翻译为连续轨迹先验（track priors）用于执行，增强高层规划与低层动作的对齐；(3) 从观测中监控任务进度并实现在线重规划，形成闭环控制。系统利用人类视频训练以减少对机器人采集数据的依赖。

Result: 在多种真实世界长时程任务中，TTS实现了74.8%的平均成功率，并支持物体级别的泛化和组合泛化能力。

Conclusion: TTS通过实例接地三元组表示和在线闭环重规划，有效解决了层级式流水线规划弱接地、弱对齐和无反馈的缺陷，验证了闭环长时程模仿学习系统在不确定环境中的可靠性和泛化能力。

Abstract: Ensuring reliability in uncertain environments remains difficult for long-horizon robotic manipulation. End-to-end VLA models are data-heavy and opaque, making diagnosis and verification difficult. Hierarchical pipelines are more interpretable, but their plans are often weakly grounded in observations, weakly aligned with low-level actions, and computed without online feedback, leading to open-loop behavior and hallucinations. To address these issues, we introduce the Triplet-to-Track System (TTS), a closed-loop long-horizon imitation learning system that uses human videos to reduce reliance on robot-collected data. TTS represents high-level subgoals as instance-grounded triplets, translates them into continuous track priors for execution, and monitors task progress from observations for online replanning. Across diverse real-world long-horizon tasks, TTS achieves a 74.8\% average success rate and supports object-level and compositional generalization.

</details>


### [153] [Privileged Critic Training Enables Sensor-Free Thruster Fault Adaptation in End-to-End RL](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.22976)
*Ricard Marsal I Castan,Miguel A. Olivares-Méndez*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出RAFT（Recurrent Asymmetric Fault Tolerant），一种带有循环记忆和特权非对称评论家的策略，通过在训练时让PPO值函数访问真实退化状态，使actor仅依赖标准任务观测即可在部署时实现无传感器故障适应。在8推进器+1反作用轮的浮动平台上，最多4个同时推进器故障下达到70.2%成功率，弥补了从无故障感知基线(4.8%)到全知oracle(82.4%)之间84%的差距。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 推进器驱动的机器人容错导航需要在线适应故障，但故障既不是二元的也不是完全可观测的——推进器可能连续退化、彻底失效或被卡在常开状态。经典的故障检测流水线需要部署时不可用的专用传感器，而能观测真实故障状态的oracle控制器同样不切实际。因此需要一种无需专用故障感知的传感器无关故障适应方法。

Method: 提出RAFT策略：采用循环记忆（recurrent memory）的PPO策略，训练时采用特权非对称评论家设计——价值函数可访问真实退化状态dgt，而actor仅接收标准任务观测。通过在训练阶段将退化状态信息注入评论家，可塑造出在部署时无需任何专用故障传感即可补偿故障的策略。

Result: 在具有8个推进器和1个反作用轮的浮动平台机器人上进行评估，在最多四个同时推进器故障的情况下，RAFT达到70.2%的成功率，相比无故障感知基线（4.8%）和能看到完整退化状态的oracle策略（82.4%），弥补了84%的性能差距。

Conclusion: 特权评论家训练足以实现无传感器的故障适应，RAFT证明了通过在训练过程中向评论家提供真实故障状态而仅让actor依靠标准观测，即可在部署时有效补偿推进器故障，无需任何专用故障传感器。代码、检查点和数据均已开源。

Abstract: Fault-tolerant navigation for thruster-actuated robots requires online adaptation to failures that are neither binary nor fully observable: thrusters may degrade continuously, fail dead, or jam stuck-open. Classical fault detection pipelines require dedicated sensors unavailable at deployment; oracle controllers that observe the true failure state are equally impractical. We show that privileged critic training is sufficient for sensor-free fault adaptation: giving the PPO value function access to the true degradation state dgt during training, while the actor receives only standard task observations, shapes a policy that compensates for failures at deployment without any dedicated fault sensing. We propose RAFT (Recurrent Asymmetric Fault Tolerant), a policy with recurrent memory trained with a privileged asymmetric critic. Evaluated on a floating-platform robot (8 thrusters, 1 reaction wheel) under up to four simultaneous thruster failures, RAFT achieves 70.2% success at four concurrent failures, closing 84% of the gap from a failure-naive baseline (4.8%) to an oracle policy that sees the full degradation state at deployment (82.4%). All code, checkpoints, and data are open-source.

</details>


### [154] [InstructMove: A Text-Indispensable Benchmark for Instruction-Following Manipulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.22990)
*Mengao Zhao,Ziang Li,Chaodong Huang,Mengchen Ma,Haoyi Jiang,Yiwei Jin,Xinjie Wang,Yun Du,Xuewu Lin,Taojun Ding,Hongyu Xie,Jackson Jiang,Chunlei Yu,Kaihua Zhang,Lichao Huang,Liu Liu,Tianwei Lin,Zhizhong Su*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出InstructMove，一个"文本不可或缺"（text-indispensable）的指令跟随操作基准。该基准确保在视觉和物理上多个动作都可行，只有与语言指令一致的动作才是正确的，从而避免策略依赖视觉捷径而非真正理解语言。实验表明该基准能诊断视觉捷径，且其仿真数据可提升真实世界的指令跟随操作性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有操作基准在评估指令跟随能力时存在不足：目标物体或目的地通常具有视觉显著性或是唯一可行的，这使得策略即使不真正理解语言指令也能成功执行。作者认为指令跟随评估应当是"文本不可或缺"的——多个动作在视觉和物理上都应可行，仅有一个与语言指令一致。

Method: 提出InstructMove基准，在拾取-放置场景中引入语义干扰物（semantic distractors），将指令跟随分解为类别识别、属性区分、空间推理和组合式拾取-放置四类任务。基准支持训练-评估协议（含InstructMove训练数据和留出评估任务），并提供语言依赖性诊断工具。

Result: 对代表性VLA策略的实验表明，InstructMove能作为受控测试平台诊断视觉捷径（visual shortcuts），并且InstructMove仿真数据能够提升真实世界的指令跟随操作性能。

Conclusion: InstructMove为指令跟随操作评估提供了更严谨的基准设计原则（文本不可或缺），不仅能有效诊断策略是否真正依赖语言理解，还能通过仿真数据迁移改善现实世界的指令跟随操作表现。

Abstract: Vision-language-action (VLA) models have made general-purpose robot manipulation increasingly plausible by conditioning robot actions on natural-language instructions. A key test of such generality is whether policies actually follow language instructions. Yet many manipulation benchmarks leave this ability underdetermined: the intended object or destination is often visually salient or uniquely feasible, allowing policies to succeed without grounding the instruction. We argue that instruction-following evaluation should be text-indispensable: multiple actions should be visually and physically plausible, while only one should be consistent with the language instruction. We introduce InstructMove, a text-indispensable benchmark for instruction-following manipulation. InstructMove instantiates this principle in pick-and-place scenes with semantic distractors, decomposing instruction following into category identification, attribute discrimination, spatial reasoning, and compositional pick-and-place. InstructMove supports a train-eval protocol with InstructMove training data and held-out evaluation tasks, with additional diagnostics for language dependence. Experiments with representative VLA policies show that InstructMove provides a controlled testbed for diagnosing visual shortcuts and that InstructMove simulation data can improve real-world instruction-following manipulation performance. Code: https://github.com/HorizonRobotics/RoboOrchardSim

</details>


### [155] [RoboRacer Arena: Scaling High-Fidelity Autonomous Racing in Isaac Sim](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.23040)
*Mihaela-Larisa Clement,Agnes Poks,Ezio Bartocci*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: RoboRacer Arena是一个从占用栅格地图自动生成3D赛车环境的系统，通过洪水填充算法提取可行驶走廊、距离场定义碰撞边界，并将赛道组装为USD stage在Isaac Sim中运行，支持从SLAM、F1赛道缩放或自然语言描述生成地图，包含130条赛道，初始化时间1.18-2.48秒，基准测试达8,707车辆步/秒。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: RoboRacer平台使用1:10比例自动驾驶车辆进行研究，但现有赛道种类有限且获取策略困难。占用栅格模拟器缺乏物理接触，而3D模拟器需要为每条赛道单独实现资源，限制了可扩展性。需要一种能从占用地图自动生成3D赛车环境的方法来解决这一瓶颈。

Method: 首先使用洪水填充算法提取可驾驶走廊并识别赛道边界以建立护栏；计算距离场定义碰撞边界；将赛道表面、碰撞属性和材质组装为USD stage，实现Isaac Sim中自动化可复现的环境生成。输入地图可来自SLAM会话、缩放的F1赛道或自然语言描述。对于自然语言输入，使用Gemma 4 31B生成赛道规格说明（不指定坐标或几何），再进行几何筛选、程序化生成和栅格级验证。

Result: 模拟环境初始化时间为1.18至2.48秒，随栅格尺寸线性增长。在30组匹配试验（10条赛道×3个种子）中生成21张地图且全部通过验证。系统包含130条赛道，支持自然语言生成。基准测试中，256个并行刚体车辆可达8,707车辆步/秒（不含渲染和策略执行时间）。

Conclusion: RoboRacer Arena通过自动化管线实现了从2D占用栅格到3D模拟环境的快速、可复现、可扩展的赛道生成，兼具模拟器的物理保真度和多赛道支持的灵活性，为自动驾驶策略获取提供了高效的基准平台。

Abstract: RoboRacer offers a standardized platform for research using 1:10-scale autonomous vehicles, but the variety of available tracks hinders the process of acquiring policies. Although existing occupancy-grid simulators allow for the quick addition of new maps, they fail to include physical contact, while 3D simulators require each circuit to be implemented as a separate asset, thus limiting their scalability. In order to overcome this issue, we have developed RoboRacer Arena, a system that creates 3D racing environments directly from occupancy maps. Our method starts by using a flood fill algorithm to extract the drivable corridors and to identify the track boundaries, which are then used to establish the barriers. A distance field is calculated to define the collision boundaries. The track surfaces, collision properties, and materials are assembled into a USD stage, which allows for the automated and reproducible generation of the environment in Isaac Sim. The input maps can be obtained from SLAM sessions, from rescaled Formula 1 circuits, or from natural-language descriptions. When the input is based on natural language, we use Gemma 4 31B to generate a track specification without specifying any coordinates or geometry. To guarantee consistency and reproducibility, we apply geometric screening, procedural generation, and raster-level validation. The simulation environments are initialized in a time range of 1.18 to 2.48 seconds, with the initialization time increasing linearly as the raster size increases. In 30 matched trials involving 10 tracks and 3 seeds, 21 maps were generated and all passed validation. RoboRacer Arena currently contains 130 tracks and supports the generation of tracks from natural language. In benchmark tests, the system attains 8,707 vehicle-steps per second when using 256 parallel rigid-body vehicles, excluding the time taken for rendering and policy execution.

</details>


### [156] [Shaping the Evolutionary Dynamics of Robot Morphology via Adaptive Control Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.23100)
*Junru Song,Yang Yang,Yaqing Xu,Ying Wen,Wei Peng,Guozhen Li,Wei'en Zhou,Wen Yao*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文通过解耦形态智能与真实潜力两个正交维度，揭示了过早的适应性评估系统性低估真实潜力并偏向快速学习者，导致所谓的形态鲍德温效应实际上是评估偏差的产物。作者提出AdaControl方法监控进化过程中对形态智能的比例失调选择并分配最小充分的控制学习量，使简单遗传算法在发现多样且高性能设计方面媲美最先进的生成式协同设计方法，同时相比穷举式控制学习节省高达80%的计算量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 已有研究确立了良好适应的形态能促进控制学习的更快收敛（即形态智能），但控制学习如何反过来塑造形态进化这一反向方向尚未被探索。作者旨在对大脑-身体耦合的双向交互进行整体性理解。

Method: 将形态对控制学习的贡献解耦为正交的两个维度：形态智能（收敛速度）与真实潜力（性能上限）。建立函数关系从个体学习曲线联合表征两个量，并在种群层面聚合捕捉进化概况。在此基础上提出AdaControl，通过监测进化中对形态智能的比例失调选择，动态分配最小充分的控制学习量以实现无偏的适应性评估。

Result: 在模拟体素软体机器人上的大量实验表明，过早的适应性评估系统性低估真实潜力并偏向快速学习者，限制了设计空间的探索，损害优化效率和形态多样性。广泛认可的形态鲍德温效应被发现是这种偏差的伪影。AdaControl使简单遗传算法在多样化高性能设计发现上可与最先进的生成式协同设计方法相媲美，同时比穷举式控制学习节省最高80%的计算开销。

Conclusion: 控制学习的评估策略对形态进化的方向和质量有深远影响；通过消除评估偏差（AdaControl），可以在不牺牲多样性与性能的前提下大幅提升计算效率，并揭示此前被误判的进化现象。

Abstract: Robot co-design via bi-level optimization couples within-lifetime controller learning for fitness evaluation with cross-generational morphological evolution. Prior work has established that well-adapted morphology facilitates faster control learning, a property termed morphological intelligence. Yet how control learning reciprocally shapes morphological evolution remains unexplored. This paper examines both directions for a holistic account of brain-body interplay. We first show that morphological contributions to control learning decouple into two orthogonal dimensions. We formalize the convergence speed as morphological intelligence and identify the performance ceiling as a complementary quantity termed true potential. A concise functional relation is then established to jointly characterize both quantities from individual learning curves, which, when aggregated at the population level, capture evolutionary profiles. Through extensive experiments on simulated voxel-based soft robots, we reveal that premature fitness evaluation systematically underestimates true potential and biases selection towards fast learners. This restricts design space exploration, compromising both optimization efficiency and morphological diversity. Notably, the widely recognized morphological Baldwin effect emerges as an artifact of this bias rather than a general evolutionary tendency. We therefore propose AdaControl, which monitors disproportionate selection for morphological intelligence during evolution and allocates minimally sufficient control learning for unbiased fitness evaluation. With AdaControl, a simple genetic algorithm rivals state-of-the-art generative-model-based co-design methods in discovering diverse high-performing designs while cutting computation by up to 80% versus exhaustive control.

</details>


### [157] [Pointing-VLA: Typed Spatial Grounding Interfaces for Vision-Language-Action Manipulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.23138)
*Xiwen Chen,Zelin Li,Zhiruo Zhou,Huiming Chen,Chenwei Wang,Xiaojun Zhu*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: Pointing-VLA 是一种构建在 Embodied-R1 之上的类型化隐藏状态空间读出方法，通过几何专用头预测归一化点、物体功能接地（OFG）热图和视觉轨迹，避免了将几何信息序列化为文本。在 Bridge/WidowX 和物理抓取放置任务中达到 SOTA 性能（平均 72.9%），且无需 Bridge 特定微调；OFG/接触读出可迁移至 NORA-1.5，保持/提升成功率的同时将控制器时间降低 20 倍以上，类型化头比文本解码快 6.68-6.90 倍；作为 π_0.5 动作策略的空间引导时，将真实机器人成功率从 52.7% 提升至 80.7%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有 VLA 模型通过自回归文本坐标或语义不透明的动作 token 暴露空间接地能力，导致多模态推理与机器人执行之间的接口脆弱且低效。

Method: 提出 Pointing-VLA，基于 Embodied-R1 构建类型化隐藏状态空间读出：几何专用头分别预测归一化点、OFG 热图和视觉轨迹，并以显式执行契约将 PICK 分配给源条件 OFG、将 PLACE 分配给 Pointing，实现阶段对齐的空间目标，不使用文本序列化。

Result: 在 Bridge/WidowX 四任务集上达到平均 72.9% 的 SOTA 性能（无 Bridge 特化微调、碰撞启用的 CuRobo 执行下）；OFG/接触读出迁移至 NORA-1.5 保持或提升成功率同时减少 20 倍以上控制器时间；类型化头比 Embodied-R1 文本解码快 6.68-6.90 倍；作为 π_0.5 的空间引导时成功率从 52.7% 提升至 80.7%。

Conclusion: 类型化空间读出可作为具身推理与机器人执行之间高效、可检查的界面，为 VLA 模型的空间接地提供了优于文本序列化的替代方案。

Abstract: Vision-language-action (VLA) models often expose spatial grounding through autoregressive text coordinates or opaque action tokens, creating brittle interfaces between multimodal reasoning and robot execution. We present Pointing-VLA, a typed hidden-state spatial readout built on Embodied-R1. Geometry-specific heads predict normalized points, object-functional grounding (OFG) heatmaps, and visual trajectories without serializing geometry as text. For the evaluated Bridge/WidowX and physical pick-place deployments, an explicit execution contract assigns PICK to source-conditioned OFG and PLACE to Pointing, providing direct stage-aligned spatial targets. Pointing-VLA achieves SOTA performance on Bridge/WidowX, averaging 72.9\% across the evaluated four-task set without Bridge-specific finetuning under collision-enabled CuRobo execution. Pointing and OFG show complementary strengths across native and cross-dataset evaluations. The OFG/contact readout transfers to NORA-1.5, preserving or improving success while reducing recorded controller time by more than 20$\times$; typed heads are also 6.68--6.90$\times$ faster than Embodied-R1 text decoding on a shared external suite. When integrated as spatial guidance for a $π_{0.5}$ action policy, Pointing-VLA raises autonomous real-robot success from 52.7\% to 80.7\% across three visual contexts. These results establish typed spatial readouts as an efficient, inspectable interface between embodied reasoning and robot execution.

</details>


<div id='eess.IV'></div>

# eess.IV [[Back]](#toc)

### [158] [Single-Model Adaptive Wireless Image Transmission via Feature Sparsity Regularization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.21743)
*Xianghao Cui,Li Lan,Qi He,Bo Che,Chenyuan Feng,Zhi Chen,Tony Q. S. Quek*

Main category: eess.IV

TL;DR: 提出TS-JSCC，一个基于尾结构稀疏化的单模型自适应联合信源信道编码（JSCC）框架，实现无线图像传输中的内容自适应特征通道分配、传输速率调整和信道SNR自适应，无需熵编码、上下文预测或显式率图等额外结构。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有JSCC方案在带宽受限和时变无线链路上，要实现内容自适应的资源分配通常依赖熵编码、上下文/概率预测、显式率图或辅助分配网络，这复杂化了编码器-解码器流程并增加边信息开销。需要一个单模型即可支持可调传输速率、适应信道条件变化且动态分配资源的简洁方案。

Method: 提出TS-JSCC框架，包含两个核心组件：(1) 基于L1的尾结构化稀疏化目标，激励每个token保留活跃特征通道前缀并抑制尾部通道，通过活跃前缀传输实现内容自适应的特征通道分配和紧凑边信息；(2) 轻量级的分阶段神经调节模块，利用归一化稀疏控制系数和信道信噪比（SNR）对中间特征进行重新缩放，实现单模型的传输速率和SNR自适应。

Result: 在CIFAR-10、Kodak和CLIC2021数据集上，分别在AWGN和Rayleigh衰落信道下进行实验，TS-JSCC在率失真性能上优于最新的学习型JSCC基线，且与理想化的分离基线相比具有竞争力，同时保持简单的单次编码器-解码器结构，无需额外结构或计算。

Conclusion: TS-JSCC通过尾结构化稀疏化和轻量级神经调节模块，在单一模型中同时实现了内容自适应通道分配、速率调节和SNR自适应，兼顾了性能与架构简洁性，为无线图像传输提供了一个高效且实用的JSCC方案。

Abstract: Learned joint source-channel coding (JSCC) enables robust wireless image transmission by jointly optimizing the transmitter and receiver over differentiable channel models. For bandwidth-limited and time-varying visual links, a single model should support user-adjustable transmission rate and adapt to changing wireless channel conditions, while also dynamically allocating resources according to spatial content. Existing content-adaptive or dynamic allocation schemes often rely on entropy coding, context/probability prediction, explicit rate maps or masks, or auxiliary allocation networks, complicating the encoder-decoder pipeline and increasing side-information overhead. We propose TS-JSCC, a single-model adaptive JSCC framework with tail-structured sparsification. First, an L1-based tail-structured sparsification objective encourages each token to retain an active feature-channel prefix while suppressing trailing ones. This enables content-adaptive feature-channel allocation with compact side information through active-prefix transmission. Second, lightweight stage-wise neural regulating modules use a normalized sparsity-control coefficient and the channel signal-to-noise ratio (SNR) to rescale intermediate features for single-model transmission rate and SNR adaptation. Experiments on CIFAR-10, Kodak, and CLIC2021 under additive white Gaussian noise (AWGN) and Rayleigh fading show that TS-JSCC achieves strong rate-distortion performance against the latest learned-JSCC baselines and remains competitive with the considered idealized separation baselines, while retaining a simple one-shot encoder-decoder without extra structures or computations.

</details>


### [159] [CRS-Bench: A Reference-Relative Reliability Benchmark for Medical Image Encoders](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.22059)
*Xingtao Lin,Hangqi Ren,Caiwan Sun,You Chen*

Main category: eess.IV

TL;DR: 本文提出CRS-Bench，一个用于医学图像编码器多目标选择的受控基准。它评估15个预训练编码器家族在三个领域（皮肤病、眼科、放射科）的四维可靠性表现（判别力、校准、标签效率、鲁棒性），并引入Pareto感知的Clinical Reliability Score（CRS）来综合排序。实验发现AUROC与CRS并非决策等价（21/105对排序反转），并识别出PanDerm、MedSigLIP和MedGemma为稳定领先的可靠性梯队。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 医学图像分类中预训练编码器选择至关重要，但仅凭干净测试集上的AUROC不足以做出全面决策——具有相似AUROC的编码器可能在校准、标签效率和对抗扰动的鲁棒性上差异显著。现有评估缺乏多维度、受控的比较框架。

Method: 构建CRS-Bench基准：覆盖3个医学领域（ISIC 2019、APTOS 2019、CheXpert）、15个编码器家族，包含CheXpert到MIMIC-CXR的机构迁移作为分布偏移场景，产生17,575条受控运行记录。每个编码器沿四个维度评估：判别力（AUROC）、校准、标签效率和鲁棒性。设计Clinical Reliability Score（CRS）作为Pareto感知、参考相对的评分，综合优势度、轮廓平衡和最差轴性能。

Result: AUROC与CRS正相关但非决策等价：105对排序中有21对反转，平均绝对排名位移为1.87。配对种子自助法分析表明PanDerm、MedSigLIP和MedGemma构成一个统计上稳定的领先可靠性梯队，而非有明确统计显著性的单一最优编码器。

Conclusion: CRS-Bench为医学图像编码器选择提供了受控的多轴可靠性评估框架，表明应从多维度可靠性剖面而非仅凭干净测试AUROC来选型，填补了现有评估方法在编码器选择决策支持上的空白。

Abstract: Pretrained image encoders are central to medical image classification, where expert annotation is costly and task-specific cohorts are often limited. As the model space expands from general-purpose to broad-medical and specialty-specific encoders, selecting the representation becomes a substantive modeling decision. Clean-test discrimination alone is insufficient for this purpose: encoders with similar AUROC can differ in calibration, label efficiency, and stability under acquisition perturbations or distribution shift.
  We introduce CRS-Bench, a controlled benchmark for multi-objective medical encoder selection. CRS-Bench evaluates 15 pretrained encoder families across dermatology, ophthalmology, and radiology using ISIC 2019, APTOS 2019, and CheXpert, with CheXpert-to-MIMIC-CXR as an observed institutional shift, yielding 17,575 controlled run records and 3,515 seed-aggregated metric rows. Each encoder is characterized along four operational reliability dimensions: discrimination, calibration, label efficiency, and robustness. We summarize these dimensions using the Clinical Reliability Score (CRS), a Pareto-aware, reference-relative score combining dominance, profile balance, and worst-axis performance.
  AUROC and CRS are positively associated but not decision-equivalent: 21 of 105 pairwise orderings reverse, with a mean absolute rank displacement of 1.87. Paired-seed bootstrap analysis identifies PanDerm, MedSigLIP, and MedGemma as a stable leading reliability tier rather than a statistically resolved single leader. CRS-Bench provides a controlled framework for selecting medical image encoders from multi-axis reliability profiles rather than clean-test AUROC alone.

</details>


### [160] [Interpretable Landsat-to-Hyperspectral Dual Super-Resolution Without Large Matrix Inversion](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.22790)
*Chia-Hsiang Lin,Jian-Kai Huang,Si-Sheng Young,Wei-Cheng Zheng*

Main category: eess.IV

TL;DR: 本文提出PAINT（PGD-ADMM可解释网络），用于将全球Landsat-8/9多光谱图像转换为AVIRIS级别高光谱图像。该任务涉及空间超分辨率（30m→15m）和光谱超分辨率（7波段→172波段）的双重超分辨率（DualSR）。PAINT利用Woodbury W-Lemma和光谱连续性先验定制ADMM-Net，并采用泛锐化策略恢复空间细节，同时通过设计无大矩阵求逆的PGD-Net解决计算瓶颈。实验表明PAINT在重建性能和计算复杂度上均显著优于现有方法，并将Landsat分类准确率从78.98%提升至92.16%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 全球高光谱图像直接采集在硬件和资源上不可行，而全球高光谱监测对遥感应用至关重要。现有光谱超分辨率方法主要面向CAVE级别的31个可见光波段，无法适用于AVIRIS级别的172个波段（含可见光、近红外和短波红外）。这促使作者为AVIRIS级别的DualSR任务定制可解释的交替方向乘子法网络。

Method: 提出PAINT网络，其核心包含：（1）基于Woodbury W-Lemma和光谱连续性先验定制的ADMM-Net，用于光谱超分辨率；（2）采用泛锐化策略而非生成式方法恢复物理可解释的空间细节；（3）针对泛锐化策略引发的大矩阵求逆（LMI）问题，设计LMI-free的近端梯度下降网络（PGD-Net）进行高效求解。

Result: PAINT在计算复杂度和重建性能两方面均取得显著提升，超越现有最先进方法。在下游应用中，PAINT将Landsat分类准确率从78.98%提升至92.16%，kappa系数从76.06%提升至90.98%。

Conclusion: 通过将ADMM-Net与PGD-Net有机结合，PAINT成功实现了从Landsat多光谱到AVIRIS级别高光谱的高质量、高效可解释重建，并在下游分类任务中展现了显著的应用价值，为全球高光谱监测提供了一条可行的技术路径。

Abstract: Direct acquisition of global hyperspectral images (HSIs) is infeasible given contemporary hardware facilities and limited resources, while global hyperspectral monitoring is critical for remote sensing applications. A more economical approach is to interpretably convert global Landsat-8/9 multispectral images into NASA's AVIRIS-level HSIs. This conversion involves both spatial super-resolution (SpaSR, 30-m to 15-m) and the highly ill-posed spectral super-resolution (SpeSR, 7-band to 172-band), collectively referred to as dual super-resolution (DualSR), whose duality between SpaSR and SpeSR has recently been established. Existing SpeSR methods, mostly designed to reconstruct CAVE-level HSIs with only 31 visible bands, are not applicable to the AVIRIS-level task involving 172 visible, near-infrared, and shortwave-infrared bands. This motivates us to customize an interpretable alternating direction method of multipliers network (ADMM-Net) using the Woodbury W-Lemma and a spectral continuity prior. Unlike conventional generative SpaSR, we employ a panchromatic sharpening strategy to recover physically grounded spatial details. However, this strategy induces very large matrix inversions (LMIs), with dimensionality proportional to the number of pixels, even after applying the W-Lemma. We resolve this issue by designing an LMI-free proximal gradient descent network (PGD-Net). Consequently, the proposed PGD-ADMM interpretable network (PAINT) achieves substantial improvements in both computational complexity and reconstruction performance. Beyond state-of-the-art reconstruction performance, PAINT improves Landsat classification from 78.98% accuracy and 76.06% kappa to 92.16% accuracy and 90.98% kappa.

</details>
